## CURRENT primary source (complete selected definitions, not executor paraphrases)


### PRIMARY experiments/verify_public_online_skill_r49.py
```
"""Reproduce R49 common-answer scores from public evidence, offline.

Only public files, semantic records and ordinary image links are required.
No provider access, private archive paths or image/file digests are used.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from cxr_online_primitives_r49 import read
from online_memory_r49 import save_once
from run_online_skill_r49 import ARM
import score_online_skill_r49 as scorer
from score_cxr_author_alignment import adapt


def asset_normalizer(evidence):
    """Resolve each document's image links to one common semantic asset name."""
    assets = []
    for index, item in enumerate(read(evidence/'image_assets.json')):
        target = (evidence/item['path']).resolve()
        if not target.is_relative_to(evidence.resolve()) or not target.is_file():
            raise ValueError('self_contained_public_asset_missing_or_outside_evidence')
        assets.append((target, 'PUBLIC_ASSET:'+str(index)))
    mappings_by_parent = {}
    def normalize(value, document):
        parent = Path(document).parent
        if parent not in mappings_by_parent:
            mappings_by_parent[parent] = sorted(
                [(os.path.relpath(target, parent).replace('\\', '/'), canonical)
                 for target, canonical in assets], key=lambda pair: -len(pair[0]))
        mappings = mappings_by_parent[parent]
        def visit(item):
            if isinstance(item, dict):
                return {key: visit(child) for key, child in item.items()}
            if isinstance(item, list):
                return [visit(child) for child in item]
            if isinstance(item, str):
                for relative, canonical in mappings:
                    item = item.replace(relative, canonical)
            return item
        return visit(value)
    return normalize


def compare_public(result, details, evidence):
    saved = read(evidence/'scores/summary.json'); previous = read(evidence/'scores/per_question.json')
    for key in ('dataset', 'arm', 'questions', 'all_questions_fresh', 'mode', 'arms', 'comparisons',
                'skill_hit_questions', 'same_image_hit_questions', 'windows', 'resources',
                'references_read_after_all_arms_adapted'):
        if saved.get(key) != result.get(key):
            raise ValueError('public_score_or_resource_changed:' + key)
    by_id = {row['question_id']: row for row in previous}
    if len(previous) != len(by_id) or set(by_id) != {row['question_id'] for row in details}:
        raise ValueError('public_scored_question_membership')
    # Archive paths are intentionally portable; answer extraction and all metric
    # decisions must remain identical after that ordinary path transformation.
    ignore = {'archive_call', 'reuse_chain'}
    for row in details:
        before = by_id[row['question_id']]
        for key in ('question_id', 'window', 'skill_hit', 'same_image_hit', 'subset', 'reference'):
            if before.get(key) != row.get(key):
                raise ValueError('public_per_question_semantics_changed:' + key)
        if set(before['arms']) != set(row['arms']):
            raise ValueError('public_per_question_arm_membership')
        for arm, value in row['arms'].items():
            old = before['arms'][arm]
            if {k: v for k, v in old.items() if k not in ignore} != {k: v for k, v in value.items() if k not in ignore}:
                raise ValueError('public_answer_adaptation_changed:' + row['question_id'] + ':' + arm)


def verify(evidence, out):
    evidence, out = Path(evidence).resolve(), Path(out).resolve()
    if out.exists() or out.is_relative_to(evidence) or evidence.is_relative_to(out):
        raise ValueError('new_verification_output_separate_from_evidence_required')
    publication = read(evidence/'publication_manifest.json')
    if (publication.get('schema') != 'r49_semantic_public_evidence_v1'
            or publication.get('scope') != 'complete_scored_online_stream'
            or publication.get('arm') != ARM or publication.get('raw_generation_exit') != 0):
        raise ValueError('full_public_stream_required_not_partial_failure_report')
    dataset = publication['dataset']
    if dataset not in scorer.EXPECTED or publication['questions'] != scorer.EXPECTED[dataset][0]:
        raise ValueError('public_fixed_dataset_denominator')
    for name in publication['files']:
        target = (evidence/name).resolve()
        if not target.is_relative_to(evidence) or not target.is_file():
            raise ValueError('public_declared_file_missing_or_outside_evidence')
    normalize = asset_normalizer(evidence)
    raw_read, raw_rows = scorer.read, scorer.rows
    def public_read(path):
        return normalize(raw_read(path), Path(path))
    def public_rows(path):
        return [normalize(row, Path(path)) for row in raw_rows(path)]

    # Load each historical answer bundle only when the scorer reaches its
    # reference-free adaptation phase. It contains actual raw terminals and
    # saved predictions, not precomputed correctness or reference labels.
    names = set(read(evidence/'scores/summary.json')['comparisons'])
    baseline_name = scorer.EXPECTED[dataset][1]
    if names not in ({baseline_name}, {baseline_name, 'R47_online_GT'}):
        raise ValueError('fixed_public_historical_comparators')
    def adapted_bundle(inputs, name):
        bundle = public_read(evidence/'comparison_sources.json')
        if set(bundle) != names:
            raise ValueError('public_historical_bundle_arm_membership')
        values = bundle[name]
        if set(values) != {row['question_id'] for row in inputs}:
            raise ValueError('public_historical_bundle_question_membership')
        return {row['question_id']: adapt(row, values[row['question_id']]['prediction'], values[row['question_id']]['source'])
                for row in inputs}
    def baseline_adapter(inputs, ds, name):
        if ds != dataset or name != baseline_name:
            raise ValueError('public_historical_baseline_identity')
        return adapted_bundle(inputs, name)
    def r47_adapter(inputs, baseline, frozen_path, run):
        return adapted_bundle(inputs, 'R47_online_GT')

    # Scoped read adapters normalize paths, not answers, labels or model
    # outputs. No execution module or historical record is modified.
    with patch.object(scorer, 'read', side_effect=public_read), \
            patch.object(scorer, 'rows', side_effect=public_rows), \
            patch.object(scorer, 'adapt_historical', side_effect=baseline_adapter), \
            patch.object(scorer, 'adapt_r47', side_effect=r47_adapter):
        secondary = 'R47_online_GT' in names
        result, details = scorer.score(evidence, evidence, evidence/'references.jsonl', dataset,
                           r47_frozen=evidence/'comparison_sources.json' if secondary else None,
                           r47_run=evidence if secondary else None)
    for qid in {row['question_id'] for row in details}:
        event = read(evidence/'feedback'/(qid+'.json')); vault = read(evidence/'reference_vault'/(qid+'.json'))
        annotation = {key: vault[key] for key in ('answer', 'answer_text') if key in vault}
        if vault.get('question_id') != qid or annotation != event['reference_annotation']:
            raise ValueError('public_actual_vault_feedback_changed')
    compare_public(result, details, evidence)
    registered = result['resources']['tools'].get('registered_cuda_failed_attempts', 0)
    if (bool(publication.get('registered_cuda_tool_retry')) != bool(registered)
            or bool(publication.get('original_failed_cache_and_selection_retained')) != bool(registered)
            or publication.get('diagnostic_output_used_as_production_result') is not False):
        raise ValueError('public_registered_historical_attempt_disclosure_changed')
    if (bool(publication.get('registered_meta_schema_repair')) != bool(result['resources']['meta_schema_repair']['registered'])
            or bool(publication.get('registered_meta_schema_repair_2')) != bool(result['resources']['meta_schema_repair_2']['registered'])
            or bool(publication.get('ordinary_mutation_fields_amendment')) != bool(result['resources']['mutation_fields_amendment']['active'])
            or publication.get('automatic_invalid_META_to_NONE') is not False):
        raise ValueError('public_exact_META_schema_repair_disclosure_changed')
    embedding_recovery = result['resources']['embedding_http_recovery']
    if (bool(publication.get('registered_embedding_http_failure')) != bool(embedding_recovery['registered'])
            or bool(publication.get('original_embedding_specific_body_unknown')) != bool(embedding_recovery['original_specific_body_unknown'])
            or publication.get('original_embedding_error_body_reconstructed_from_diagnostic') is not False):
        raise ValueError('public_exact_embedding_HTTP_failure_disclosure_changed')
    if (bool(publication.get('registered_phrase_guard_retry')) != bool(result['resources']['phrase_guard_retry']['registered'])
            or bool(publication.get('declared_phrase_generation_policy_amendment')) != bool(result['resources']['phrase_guard_amendment']['active'])
            or publication.get('same_MAIRA_generation_settings_claim') is not False
            or publication.get('phrase_diagnostic_output_reused_in_R49') is not False):
        raise ValueError('public_phrase_guard_policy_amendment_disclosure_changed')
    if (bool(publication.get('registered_assessment_adoption_repair')) != bool(result['resources']['adoption_repair']['registered'])
            or bool(publication.get('analysis_unique_adoption_amendment')) != bool(result['resources']['analysis_unique_amendment']['active'])
            or publication.get('original_analysis_changed_for_mutation') is not False
            or publication.get('automatic_future_adoption_repair') is not False
            or publication.get('invalid_adoption_silently_ignored') is not False):
        raise ValueError('public_assess_only_adoption_provenance_disclosure_changed')
    result.update(scope='Public common-answer score reproduction and semantic evidence consistency only.',
                  matches_published_primary_scores_and_extractions=True,
                  matches_published_resources=True,
                  private_run_paths_required=False, image_or_file_digests_checked=False,
                  new_inference_calls=0, clinical_or_causal_verification=False)
    save_once(out/'summary.json', result); save_once(out/'per_question.json', details)
    return result


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--evidence', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--out', type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps(verify(args.evidence, args.out), ensure_ascii=False))

```


### PRIMARY local_assets/score_completed_r49_dataset_once.py
```
"""Score one fully finished R49 dataset once; final publication gates are unchanged."""
import argparse
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT/'experiments'))
import score_online_skill_r49 as scoring


def read(path):
    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))


def exclusive_json(path, value):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open('x', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
        json.dump(value, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        handle.write('\n')


def raw_dataset_completion(run, jobs, runner_exit, dataset):
    exits = [run/'generation.exitcode', jobs/(dataset+'.exitcode'), runner_exit]
    if runner_exit.resolve() == exits[1].resolve() or runner_exit.name != dataset+'.exitcode':
        raise ValueError('separate_actual_dataset_runner_exit_record_required')
    for path in exits:
        if not path.is_file() or path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').strip() != '0':
            raise ValueError('dataset_raw_exit_zero_required:'+str(path))
    # complete.json contains learned state: do not open it before native score's
    # reference-free adaptation. Native score validates its content afterwards.
    if not (run/'complete.json').is_file():
        raise ValueError('dataset_completion_marker_required')
    return [{'path': str(path.resolve()), 'raw_exit': 0} for path in exits]


def pre_adaptation_recovery_proof(identity, receipt, *, require_unscored):
    """Only the recorded first CheX image-alias mismatch, never an unknown retry."""
    fixed_receipt = ROOT/'local_assets/r49_dataset_score_receipts/chexbench.json'
    if identity['dataset'] != 'chexbench' or receipt.resolve() != fixed_receipt.resolve():
        raise ValueError('only_named_first_CheX_pre_adaptation_failure_can_resume')
    started_path = receipt.with_name('chexbench.started.json')
    failure_path = receipt.with_name('chexbench.pre_adaptation_failure_001.json')
    old = read(started_path); failure = read(failure_path)
    old_identity = old.get('identity', {})
    old_data = ROOT/'reports/cxr_vqa_extension/results/chexbench'
    run = Path(identity['run']); expected_data = run.parent.parent/'dataset_completion/chexbench/source_data'
    if (old.get('schema') != 'r49_dataset_score_started_v1'
            or Path(old_identity.get('data', '')).resolve() != old_data.resolve()
            or identity['data'] != str(expected_data.resolve())
            or {k: v for k, v in old_identity.items() if k != 'data'} != {k: v for k, v in identity.items() if k != 'data'}
            or old.get('new_inference_calls') != 0
            or old.get('controller_exit_zero_required_for_this_interim_score') is not False):
        raise ValueError('pre_adaptation_resume_changes_more_than_known_data_alias')
    known_failure = {'schema': 'r49_dataset_score_pre_adaptation_failure_v1', 'dataset': 'chexbench',
        'started_receipt': 'local_assets/r49_dataset_score_receipts/chexbench.started.json',
        'actual_cli_exit': 1, 'exec_chunk_id': 'e005ad', 'exception_type': 'ValueError',
        'exception_message': 'manifest_semantic_input_order_changed', 'all_arm_adaptation_executed': False,
        'references_or_feedback_or_complete_body_opened': False, 'result_directory_exists': False,
        'completed_score_receipt_exists': False, 'native_scorer_or_metrics_modified': False,
        'original_started_receipt_retained': True, 'no_automatic_rescore': True}
    if (any(failure.get(k) != v for k, v in known_failure.items())
            or not any('completed_gate' in line for line in failure.get('trace_stage', []))
            or not any('raise ValueError(manifest_semantic_input_order_changed)' in line for line in failure.get('trace_stage', []))):
        raise ValueError('only_actual_closed_prereference_input_gate_failure_is_supported')
    if require_unscored and (receipt.exists() or Path(identity['out']).exists()):
        raise ValueError('pre_adaptation_recovery_requires_no_score_outputs_or_completed_receipt')
    source_path = expected_data.parent/'SOURCE_INPUT_PULL_RECEIPT.json'; source = read(source_path)
    expected_source = {'schema': 'r49_canonical_input_source_pull_v1', 'dataset': 'chexbench',
        'status': 'canonical_input_copied_and_manifest_equal',
        'remote_source': '/home/imss/cw/MedRAX_reproduction_20261027/code_cxr_vqa_extension/data/chexbench/inputs.jsonl',
        'questions': 618, 'manifest_inputs_equal': True, 'manifest_order_equal': True,
        'references_requested': False, 'references_parsed': False, 'completion_body_parsed': False,
        'generation_or_predictions_modified': False, 'shared_download_inventory_used': False,
        'model_api_calls': 0, 'hashes_computed': False}
    if (any(source.get(k) != v for k, v in expected_source.items())
            or Path(source.get('local_data', '')).resolve() != expected_data.resolve()
            or Path(source.get('local_input', '')).resolve() != (expected_data/'inputs.jsonl').resolve()):
        raise ValueError('actual_exact_canonical_source_input_pull_required')
    inputs = lambda path: [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').splitlines() if line.strip()]
    canonical = inputs(expected_data/'inputs.jsonl'); previous = inputs(old_data/'inputs.jsonl')
    manifest = read(run/'manifest.json'); ids = [row['question_id'] for row in canonical]
    if (len(canonical) != 618 or len(previous) != 618 or len(set(ids)) != 618
            or canonical != manifest['inputs'] or ids != [item['question_id'] for item in manifest['order']]
            or [row['question_id'] for row in previous] != ids):
        raise ValueError('canonical_618_inputs_and_declared_order_must_match_without_relaxation')
    for before, after in zip(previous, canonical):
        if (set(before) != set(after) or {k: v for k, v in before.items() if k != 'images'} != {k: v for k, v in after.items() if k != 'images'}
                or before.get('images') == after.get('images') or not isinstance(before.get('images'), list)
                or not isinstance(after.get('images'), list) or len(before['images']) != len(after['images']) or not after['images']
                or any(not isinstance(name, str) or not name.startswith('assets/') for name in before['images'])
                or any(not isinstance(name, str) or not name.startswith('images/') for name in after['images'])):
            raise ValueError('only_published_assets_to_canonical_images_path_difference_supported')
    return {'schema': 'r49_fixed_chexbench_pre_adaptation_recovery_v1', 'dataset': 'chexbench',
        'original_started_path': str(started_path.resolve()), 'original_started_record': old,
        'failure_metadata_path': str(failure_path.resolve()), 'failure_metadata': failure,
        'source_pull_receipt_path': str(source_path.resolve()), 'source_pull_receipt': source,
        'canonical_inputs_equal_manifest': True, 'canonical_order_equal_manifest': True,
        'all_other_input_fields_equal': True, 'changed_field_counts': {'images': 618},
        'original_records_overwritten_or_deleted': False, 'reference_labels_parsed_by_recovery': False,
        'native_gate_or_adaptation_modified': False}


def score_once(data, run, jobs, runner_exit, references, out, dataset, *,
               r47_frozen=None, r47_run=None, receipt=None, score_backend=None, recover_pre_adaptation_failure=False):
    if dataset not in scoring.EXPECTED:
        raise ValueError('unknown_dataset')
    data, run, jobs, runner_exit, references, out = map(Path, (data, run, jobs, runner_exit, references, out))
    receipt = Path(receipt) if receipt is not None else ROOT/'local_assets/r49_dataset_score_receipts'/(dataset+'.json')
    if (r47_frozen is None) != (r47_run is None) or (dataset == 'chexbench') != (r47_run is not None):
        raise ValueError('CheX_requires_fixed_R47_secondary_to_reuse_final_scores_others_do_not')
    raw_exits = raw_dataset_completion(run, jobs, runner_exit, dataset)
    identity = {'dataset': dataset, **{name: str(value.resolve()) for name, value in {
        'data': data, 'run': run, 'jobs': jobs, 'runner_exit': runner_exit, 'references': references, 'out': out}.items()},
        'r47_frozen': str(Path(r47_frozen).resolve()) if r47_frozen is not None else None,
        'r47_run': str(Path(r47_run).resolve()) if r47_run is not None else None}
    n, baseline, expected_baseline = scoring.EXPECTED[dataset]
    if receipt.is_file():
        saved = read(receipt)
        if (saved.get('schema') != 'r49_completed_dataset_score_receipt_v1' or saved.get('status') != 'completed'
                or saved.get('identity') != identity):
            raise ValueError('existing_score_receipt_identity_or_completion_changed')
        if saved.get('native_score_invocations') == 2:
            proof = pre_adaptation_recovery_proof(identity, receipt, require_unscored=False)
            resume = read(receipt.with_name('chexbench.pre_adaptation_resume_001.started.json'))
            if (saved.get('pre_adaptation_recovery') != proof or resume.get('pre_adaptation_recovery') != proof
                    or resume.get('identity') != identity or saved.get('completed_metric_calculations') != 1
                    or saved.get('pre_adaptation_failed_invocations') != 1):
                raise ValueError('completed_resumed_score_requires_the_same_exact_historical_proof')
        elif (saved.get('native_score_invocations') != 1 or saved.get('completed_metric_calculations') != 1
                or saved.get('pre_adaptation_failed_invocations') != 0):
            raise ValueError('unexpected_score_attempt_counts')
        summary = read(out/'summary.json'); details = read(out/'per_question.json')
        if (summary != saved['summary_snapshot'] or len(details) != n
                or [item['question_id'] for item in details] != saved['scored_question_ids']):
            raise ValueError('saved_score_outputs_no_longer_match_once_only_receipt')
        return {'dataset': dataset, 'reused_completed_score': True, 'native_score_invoked_now': False,
                'score_output': str(out.resolve()), 'receipt': str(receipt.resolve()), 'summary': summary,
                'final_audit_or_publication_complete': False}
    if out.exists():
        raise ValueError('unreceipted_existing_score_output_inspect_without_rescoring')
    if (out.resolve().is_relative_to(run.resolve()) or run.resolve().is_relative_to(out.resolve())
            or receipt.resolve().is_relative_to(out.resolve()) or receipt.resolve().is_relative_to(run.resolve())):
        raise ValueError('score_output_and_private_receipt_must_be_separate_from_generation')
    started = receipt.with_name(receipt.stem+'.started.json')
    proof = None
    if recover_pre_adaptation_failure:
        proof = pre_adaptation_recovery_proof(identity, receipt, require_unscored=True)
        started = receipt.with_name('chexbench.pre_adaptation_resume_001.started.json')
    if started.exists():
        raise ValueError('score_already_started_or_interrupted_inspect_without_automatic_rescore')
    began = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    exclusive_json(started, {'schema': 'r49_dataset_score_started_v1', 'identity': identity,
        'started_at': began, 'actual_raw_exit_records': raw_exits,
        'controller_exit_zero_required_for_this_interim_score': False, 'new_inference_calls': 0,
        **({'explicit_pre_adaptation_resume': True, 'pre_adaptation_recovery': proof,
            'native_score_attempt_ordinal': 2, 'automatic_retry': False} if proof else {})})
    # This unchanged native function checks the full fixed cohort, every saved
    # artifact/request/tool/recovery proof and raw exit. It adapts ALL current
    # and historical arms before opening feedback/reference annotations.
    backend = score_backend or scoring.score
    summary, details = backend(data, run, references, dataset,
                              r47_frozen=r47_frozen, r47_run=r47_run)
    ids = [item['question_id'] for item in details]
    expected_arms = {scoring.ARM, baseline} | ({'R47_online_GT'} if dataset == 'chexbench' else set())
    if (summary.get('dataset') != dataset or summary.get('arm') != scoring.ARM or summary.get('questions') != n
            or summary.get('all_questions_fresh') is not True or summary.get('references_read_after_all_arms_adapted') is not True
            or len(details) != n or len(set(ids)) != n or set(summary.get('arms', {})) != expected_arms
            or summary['arms'][baseline]['correct'] != expected_baseline
            or dataset == 'chexbench' and summary['arms']['R47_online_GT']['correct'] != 468):
        raise ValueError('native_complete_fixed_arm_scores_required')
    if proof and pre_adaptation_recovery_proof(identity, receipt, require_unscored=True) != proof:
        raise ValueError('original_failure_or_source_record_changed_during_explicit_resume')
    scoring.save_once(out/'summary.json', summary); scoring.save_once(out/'per_question.json', details)
    record = {'schema': 'r49_completed_dataset_score_receipt_v1', 'status': 'completed', 'identity': identity,
        'started_at': began, 'completed_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        'actual_raw_exit_records': raw_exits, 'questions': n, 'scored_question_ids': ids,
        'native_score_function': 'experiments.score_online_skill_r49.score', 'native_score_invocations': 2 if proof else 1,
        'completed_metric_calculations': 1, 'pre_adaptation_failed_invocations': 1 if proof else 0,
        **({'pre_adaptation_recovery': proof} if proof else {}),
        'summary_snapshot': summary, 'references_read_after_all_arms_adapted': True,
        'new_inference_calls': 0, 'interim_completed_dataset_report': True,
        'audit_status': 'completed_dataset_offline_score_final_audit_pending',
        'online_ground_truth_feedback': True, 'causal_or_official_SOTA_claim': False,
        'later_dataset_policy_frozen_do_not_tune_from_interim_scores': True,
        'controller_exit_zero_required_for_this_interim_score': False,
        'final_three_dataset_audit_and_publication_gates_unchanged': True,
        'final_audit_or_publication_complete': False,
        'reuse_instruction': 'Reuse these exact summary/per_question files during final export/writer; do not run private scoring again. Public independent reproduction remains required.'}
    exclusive_json(receipt, record)
    return {'dataset': dataset, 'reused_completed_score': False, 'native_score_invoked_now': True,
            'score_output': str(out.resolve()), 'receipt': str(receipt.resolve()), 'summary': summary,
            'final_audit_or_publication_complete': False}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--dataset', choices=scoring.EXPECTED, required=True)
    for name in ('data', 'run', 'jobs', 'runner-exit', 'references', 'out'):
        parser.add_argument('--'+name, type=Path, required=True)
    for name in ('r47-frozen', 'r47-run', 'receipt'):
        parser.add_argument('--'+name, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--recover-pre-adaptation-failure', action='store_true',
                        help='Explicitly resume ONLY the recorded first CheX image-alias input-gate failure, once.')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    result = score_once(args.data, args.run, args.jobs, args.runner_exit, args.references, args.out, args.dataset,
        r47_frozen=args.r47_frozen, r47_run=args.r47_run, receipt=args.receipt,
        recover_pre_adaptation_failure=args.recover_pre_adaptation_failure)
    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__': main()

```


### PRIMARY local_assets/test_r49_reporting_provenance.py
```
"""Synthetic control-record integration only; no target answers, labels or scorer."""
import copy
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch


spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('r49_future_report_writer', Path(__file__).with_name('finalize_r49_report.py'))
writer = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(writer)


class ReportingProvenance(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name)/'project'
        self.report = self.root/'reports/r49'
        self.archive = Path(self.tmp.name)/'archive'
        self.prep = self.root/'local_assets/prep'
        self.receipts = self.root/'local_assets/score_receipts'
        self.public = self.report/'deployment_source/reporting_provenance'
        self.public.mkdir(parents=True)
        self.addCleanup(patch.stopall)
        patch.object(writer, 'ROOT', self.root).start()
        patch.object(writer, 'REPORT', self.report).start()
        self.bundles = {}; self.saved = {}
        self.pair(self.prep/'DATASET_COMPLETION_REPORTING_AUTHORIZATION.json', 'DATASET_COMPLETION_REPORTING_AUTHORIZATION.json', {
            'enabled': True, 'confirmation_source': 'explicit_user_reply',
            'exact_user_reply': '848题是这个三个数据集吧，可以做完一个数据集汇报一个数据集的准确率等成果',
            'datasets': {ds: writer.SPEC[ds]['n'] for ds in writer.DATASETS}, 'report_on_each_dataset_completion': True,
            'partial_dataset_scoring_allowed': False, 'reuse_saved_dataset_scores_at_finalization': True,
            'future_inference_tuned_from_report': False, 'final_all_three_exit_zero_gate_unchanged': True})
        for ds in writer.DATASETS:
            n = writer.SPEC[ds]['n']; ids = [ds+'_'+str(index) for index in range(n)]
            # There are no answers, references or learned contents in a fixture.
            summary = {'dataset': ds, 'questions': n, 'resources': {'unknown_cost_requests': 1}}
            self.bundles[ds] = {'summary': summary, 'details': [{'question_id': qid} for qid in ids]}
            identity = {'dataset': ds, 'run': str(self.archive/'generation'/ds), 'jobs': str(self.archive/'jobs'),
                'runner_exit': str(self.archive/'recovery/adoption_1'/(ds+'.exitcode')), 'out': str(self.report/'results'/ds),
                'data': str(self.archive/'dataset_completion/chexbench/source_data') if ds == 'chexbench' else str(self.root/'data'/ds),
                'references': str(self.root/'historical_references'/(ds+'.jsonl')), 'r47_frozen': None, 'r47_run': None}
            failed = int(ds == 'chexbench')
            receipt = {'schema': 'r49_completed_dataset_score_receipt_v1', 'status': 'completed', 'identity': identity,
                'questions': n, 'scored_question_ids': ids, 'summary_snapshot': summary,
                'native_score_invocations': 1+failed, 'completed_metric_calculations': 1, 'pre_adaptation_failed_invocations': failed,
                'new_inference_calls': 0, 'references_read_after_all_arms_adapted': True,
                'later_dataset_policy_frozen_do_not_tune_from_interim_scores': True,
                'actual_raw_exit_records': [{'path': str(path), 'raw_exit': 0} for path in (
                    self.archive/'generation'/ds/'generation.exitcode', self.archive/'jobs'/(ds+'.exitcode'),
                    self.archive/'recovery/adoption_1'/(ds+'.exitcode'))]}
            started = {'schema': 'r49_dataset_score_started_v1', 'identity': copy.deepcopy(identity), 'new_inference_calls': 0}
            if ds == 'chexbench':
                started['identity']['data'] = str(self.root/'published_alias_inputs')
                failure = {'schema': 'r49_dataset_score_pre_adaptation_failure_v1', 'actual_cli_exit': 1,
                    'exec_chunk_id': 'e005ad', 'exception_message': 'manifest_semantic_input_order_changed',
                    'all_arm_adaptation_executed': False, 'references_or_feedback_or_complete_body_opened': False,
                    'result_directory_exists': False, 'completed_score_receipt_exists': False,
                    'native_scorer_or_metrics_modified': False, 'original_started_receipt_retained': True}
                source = {'schema': 'r49_canonical_input_source_pull_v1', 'status': 'canonical_input_copied_and_manifest_equal',
                    'questions': n, 'manifest_inputs_equal': True, 'manifest_order_equal': True, 'references_parsed': False,
                    'model_api_calls': 0, 'hashes_computed': False, 'generation_or_predictions_modified': False,
                    'local_data': identity['data'], 'remote_source': '/remote/original/inputs.jsonl'}
                proof = {'schema': 'r49_fixed_chexbench_pre_adaptation_recovery_v1', 'original_started_record': started,
                    'failure_metadata': failure, 'source_pull_receipt': source, 'canonical_inputs_equal_manifest': True,
                    'canonical_order_equal_manifest': True, 'all_other_input_fields_equal': True, 'changed_field_counts': {'images': n},
                    'original_records_overwritten_or_deleted': False, 'native_gate_or_adaptation_modified': False}
                receipt['pre_adaptation_recovery'] = proof
                self.pair(self.receipts/'chexbench.pre_adaptation_failure_001.json', 'score_receipts/chexbench.pre_adaptation_failure_001.json', failure)
                self.pair(self.receipts/'chexbench.pre_adaptation_resume_001.started.json', 'score_receipts/chexbench.pre_adaptation_resume_001.started.json', {
                    'identity': identity, 'pre_adaptation_recovery': proof, 'explicit_pre_adaptation_resume': True,
                    'native_score_attempt_ordinal': 2, 'automatic_retry': False})
                self.pair(self.archive/'dataset_completion/chexbench/SOURCE_INPUT_PULL_RECEIPT.json',
                          'dataset_completion/chexbench/SOURCE_INPUT_PULL_RECEIPT.json', source)
            self.saved[ds] = copy.deepcopy(receipt)
            self.pair(self.receipts/(ds+'.json'), 'score_receipts/'+ds+'.json', receipt)
            self.pair(self.receipts/(ds+'.started.json'), 'score_receipts/'+ds+'.started.json', started)
            self.pair(self.archive/'dataset_completion'/ds/'PULL_RECEIPT.json', 'dataset_completion/'+ds+'/PULL_RECEIPT.json', {
                'status': 'complete_immutable_dataset_pulled', 'dataset': ds, 'questions': n, 'archive': str(self.archive),
                'references_parsed': False, 'completion_marker_body_parsed': False, 'other_dataset_predictions_pulled': False,
                'final_pull_must_preserve_this_generation_directory': True})
        self.pair(self.archive/'dataset_completion/FINAL_CONTROL_PULL_RECEIPT.json', 'dataset_completion/FINAL_CONTROL_PULL_RECEIPT.json', {
            'status': 'final_controls_pulled', 'generation_directories_skipped': list(writer.DATASETS),
            'scores_invoked': False, 'result_files_overwritten': False})
        self.pair(self.archive/'actual_code_source/source_completed.json', 'supplemental_receipts/source_completed.json', {
            'status': 'actual_code_source_copied', 'remote_execution': False, 'model_api_calls': 0,
            'byte_or_pixel_or_hash_checks': False, 'files': ['source_a.py', 'source_b.py']})
        self.pair(self.archive/'supplemental_receipts/local_captures_completed.json', 'supplemental_receipts/local_captures_completed.json', {
            'status': 'known_local_captures_preserved', 'remote_requests': 0, 'generation_writes': False,
            'capture_or_diagnostic_reexecuted': False, 'copied_missing': ['old_failure'],
            'existing_preserved_without_reading_or_overwriting': ['old_diagnostic']})

    def pair(self, source, relative, value):
        for path in (source, self.public/relative):
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            path.write_text(json.dumps(value), encoding='utf-8')

    def inspect(self):
        return writer.offline_scoring_provenance(self.archive, self.prep, self.receipts, self.public, self.bundles)

    def test_saved_metrics_reused_and_separate_reference_source_is_reported(self):
        result = self.inspect()
        self.assertEqual((result['total_native_score_function_entries'], result['completed_metric_calculations'],
                          result['pre_adaptation_failed_invocations']), (4, 3, 1))
        self.assertFalse(result['scores_or_inference_reexecuted_by_writer'])
        actual = writer.recorded_scoring_sources({}, result)
        self.assertEqual(actual['chexbench_references'], self.saved['chexbench']['identity']['references'])
        self.assertNotEqual(Path(actual['chexbench_references']).parent, Path(actual['chexbench_data']))

    def test_mutated_saved_summary_is_not_accepted_as_the_once_score(self):
        changed = copy.deepcopy(self.saved['slake']); changed['summary_snapshot']['resources']['unknown_cost_requests'] = 0
        self.pair(self.receipts/'slake.json', 'score_receipts/slake.json', changed)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'saved_completed_metric_must_match'):
            self.inspect()

    def test_erased_failed_attempt_and_guessed_reference_path_are_rejected(self):
        changed = copy.deepcopy(self.saved['chexbench']); changed['native_score_invocations'] = 1
        self.pair(self.receipts/'chexbench.json', 'score_receipts/chexbench.json', changed)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'saved_completed_metric_must_match'):
            self.inspect()
        self.pair(self.receipts/'chexbench.json', 'score_receipts/chexbench.json', self.saved['chexbench'])
        result = self.inspect()
        wrong = str(Path(self.saved['chexbench']['identity']['data'])/'references.jsonl')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'explicit_report_source_must_match'):
            writer.recorded_scoring_sources({'chexbench_references': wrong}, result)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

```

"""User-directed evidence-first publication; the final-audit publisher is unchanged."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import publish_cxr_online_skill_r49 as pub
import finalize_r49_report as writer

pub.RECEIPTS = pub.ROOT / 'local_assets/r49_evidence_first_publication'


def preliminary_ready():
    result = {}
    for ds, spec in pub.DATASETS.items():
        evidence = pub.REPORT / 'evidence' / ds
        manifest = pub.read(evidence / 'publication_manifest.json')
        saved = pub.read(pub.REPORT / 'results' / ds / 'summary.json')
        exported = pub.read(evidence / 'scores/summary.json')
        if (manifest['scope'] != 'complete_scored_online_stream'
                or manifest['questions'] != spec['n'] or manifest['raw_generation_exit'] != 0
                or saved != exported or saved['questions'] != spec['n']
                or saved['arms'][spec['baseline']]['correct'] != spec['baseline_correct']):
            raise ValueError('complete_saved_result_and_public_export_required:' + ds)
        for name in ('controller', ds):
            if (evidence / 'job_records' / (name + '.exitcode')).read_text().strip() != '0':
                raise ValueError('actual_final_zero_required:' + ds)
        result[ds] = {'n': spec['n'], 'R49_correct': saved['arms'][pub.ARM]['correct'],
                      'publication_phase': 'user_requested_evidence_first',
                      'final_DeepSeek_audit_not_claimed': True}
    if not (pub.REPORT / 'evidence/recovery/publication_manifest.json').is_file():
        raise ValueError('all_preserved_recovery_history_required')
    return result


def prepare_documents():
    preliminary_ready()
    if (pub.REPORT / 'README.md').exists():
        raise ValueError('initial_report_already_written_do_not_overwrite')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    amendment = f'''# User-directed publication sequence amendment

Recorded {now}. The actual user first said “先上传github吧”, then “抓紧推送”. These instructions supersede the earlier order requiring the final DeepSeek review before the first upload. Publish the completed results and full exported evidence first, then finish public reproduction, the one new complete-results DeepSeek review, final report and follow-up publication. This changes publication order only; it does not claim that the deferred review happened.

All three generation streams, private scores and public exports are complete. Original predictions, references, native scoring semantics, failure records and the user's open_questions_for_later.jsonl are untouched. No inference, private rescoring, transfer or old review is repeated. The original final-audit publisher and its gates remain unchanged; the separate published evidence-first helper reuses its explicit allowlist, credential/link/file-size checks, ordinary commit/push and main verification with a truthful completed-evidence gate. No force, reset or merge is authorized. Expected initial main: 641746b8680c3b62d655abb154ab773cd57e6388. A subsequent final publication may advance only the verified commit created by this authorized first upload; any other remote change stops work.

The original adoption prelaunch audit_deferred_until_full_generation control remains historical and unchanged. This amendment is not a positive result review, clinical audit or model-weight attestation.
'''
    (pub.REPORT / 'PUBLICATION_SEQUENCE_AMENDMENT.md').write_text(amendment, encoding='utf-8')
    lines = ['# R49: completed results and public evidence', '',
             '**Evidence-first publication at the user’s explicit request. Final DeepSeek result/claim review and final report are pending.**', '',
             'All 848 question-level generations and three private scores completed. Scores below reuse the immutable saved results. This upload preserves negative findings, actual failures and recovery history. It does not assert that 848 questions are independent patients or cases.', '',

             '|---|---:|---:|---:|']
    bundles = {}
    for ds, spec in pub.DATASETS.items():
        s = pub.read(pub.REPORT / 'results' / ds / 'summary.json'); bundles[ds] = {'summary': s}
        c = s['comparisons'][spec['baseline']]
        lines.append(f"| {ds} | {s['arms'][pub.ARM]['correct']}/{spec['n']} | {spec['baseline']} {spec['baseline_correct']}/{spec['n']} | {c['corrected']} / {c['harmed']} / {c['net']:+d} |")
    lines += ['', 'VQA-RAD uses M42: the M43 phase retained M42’s 100/116 and did not run a separate VQA arm. CheX R47 online-GT 468/618 remains a secondary comparator in its saved results.', '',
              'Final adoption_1 controller and all three original runners exited zero. Every initial/intermediate exit1 remains in the full recovery tree. No failed attempt is relabeled successful.', '',
              '## Evidence and current reproduction status', '']
    for ds in pub.DATASETS:
        q = pub.REPORT / 'public_reproduction' / ds / 'summary.json'
        status = 'completed: saved public scores, extractions and resources match' if q.is_file() else 'running; no completed public reproduction asserted'
        lines += [f"- **{ds}** — {status}. [Saved scores](results/{ds}/summary.json), [all question-level results](results/{ds}/per_question.json), [questions/options/images/requests/replies/tools/feedback/Skills](evidence/{ds}/), [evidence manifest](evidence/{ds}/publication_manifest.json)."]
    lines += ['', '[Full failure and recovery evidence](evidence/recovery/) · [Executed code and helper provenance](deployment_source/PROVENANCE.md) · [Protocol](PROTOCOL.json) · [Method and limitations](METHOD_AND_LIMITS.md) · [Actual test history](TESTS.md) · [Publication sequence amendment](PUBLICATION_SEQUENCE_AMENDMENT.md).', '',
              '## Interpretation and resource limits', '',

It has no simultaneous no-Skill control. Skill hits are 505/618, 0/114, 0/116; same-image memory hits are 130/618, 84/114, 58/116. SLAKE and CheX-SLAKE share 17 images / 64 matched questions; VQA paraphrases cluster by image. No Skill-only causality, equal-budget gain, closed-test SOTA or medical capability is claimed.', '',

It changes publication order, preserves the original final-audit publisher unchanged and does not fabricate a completed review. No source hash or new experiment is asserted.\n', encoding='utf-8')

### PRIMARY local_assets/repair_r49_vqarad_public_image_links.py
```
"""Repair only inherited original-image URL bases in the public VQA comparator."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REPORT = ROOT / 'reports/cxr_online_skill_r49_20261108'
EVIDENCE = REPORT / 'evidence/vqarad'
RECEIPT = REPORT / 'deployment_source/VQARAD_IMAGE_LINK_REPAIR.json'

if RECEIPT.exists():
    raise RuntimeError('portable_link_repair_already_completed')
source = EVIDENCE / 'comparison_sources.json'
inputs = {r['question_id']: r for r in (json.loads(line) for line in (EVIDENCE / 'inputs.jsonl').read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines() if line.strip())}
value = json.loads(source.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
pattern = re.compile(r'(?:\.\./)+data/vqarad_cxr/assets/([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\.(?:jpg|jpeg|png|webp))')
changes = []
for arm, cases in value.items():
    for qid, case in cases.items():
        names = {Path(p).name for p in inputs[qid]['images']}
        def portable(item):
            if isinstance(item, dict):
                return {k: portable(v) for k, v in item.items()}
            if isinstance(item, list):
                return [portable(v) for v in item]
            if not isinstance(item, str):
                return item
            def replace(match):
                name = match.group(1)
                if name not in names or not (EVIDENCE / 'assets' / name).is_file():
                    raise ValueError('original_image_filename_not_bound_to_current_question:' + qid)
                new = 'assets/' + name
                changes.append({'arm': arm, 'question_id': qid, 'old': match.group(0), 'portable': new})
                return new
            return pattern.sub(replace, item)
        cases[qid] = portable(case)
if not changes:
    raise RuntimeError('no_observed_legacy_image_base_to_repair')
backup = REPORT / 'deployment_source/vqarad_comparison_sources.before_image_link_repair.json'
if backup.exists():
    raise RuntimeError('original_public_bundle_backup_already_exists')
shutil.copyfile(source, backup)
source.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
RECEIPT.write_text(json.dumps({'scope': 'Portable image URL-base repair only, using exact question_id/current-original filename membership and already exported image existence.', 'original_public_bundle': backup.relative_to(REPORT).as_posix(), 'changes': changes, 'original_private_predictions_and_scores_modified': False, 'new_inference_or_scoring_calls': 0, 'hash_or_pixel_checks': False}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
shutil.copyfile(__file__, REPORT / 'deployment_source/repair_r49_vqarad_public_image_links.py')
print(json.dumps({'portable_image_references_repaired': len(changes), 'new_inference_or_scoring_calls': 0}))

```


### PRIMARY local_assets/aris_deepseek_readonly_review.py
```
"""Fresh/continued user-selected DeepSeek Claude Code review, isolated settings."""
import argparse,json,os,subprocess,uuid
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from deepseek_review_credential import user_supplied_key
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def put(path,value):path.write_text(json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')


def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--trace',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--prompt',type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument('--resume');a=p.parse_args();trace=a.trace.resolve();trace.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    assert not (trace/'started.json').exists(),'review_already_dispatched_do_not_retry_unknown'
    route=subprocess.run(['python','-X','utf8','C:/Users/cheng/aris_repo/tools/reviewer_route.py','--project',str(ROOT),
          '--host','codex','--tier','deep','--reviewer','claude-code','--model','deepseek-v4-pro'],capture_output=True,text=True,encoding='utf-8',check=True)
    (trace/'route.json').write_text(route.stdout,encoding='utf-8')
    cfg=json.loads(Path('C:/Users/cheng/.claude/settings.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'));env=os.environ.copy()
    for key in list(env):
        if key.startswith('ANTHROPIC_'):env.pop(key)
    env['ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL']=cfg['env']['ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL'];env['ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN']=user_supplied_key()
    for name in ['ANTHROPIC_MODEL','ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_OPUS_MODEL','ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL','ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL']:
        env[name]='deepseek-v4-pro'
    cli=Path(os.environ['APPDATA'])/'npm/node_modules/@anthropic-ai/claude-code/bin/claude.exe';assert cli.is_file()
    session=a.resume or str(uuid.uuid4());prompt=a.prompt.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    (trace/'request.md').write_text(prompt,encoding='utf-8')
    args=[str(cli),'-p','--safe-mode','--setting-sources','','--model','deepseek-v4-pro',
       '--tools','Read,Grep,Glob','--allowedTools','Read,Grep,Glob','--permission-mode','dontAsk','--no-chrome','--output-format','json']
    args+=['--resume',session] if a.resume else ['--session-id',session]
    put(trace/'started.json',{'utc':datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(),'session_id':session,'fresh':not bool(a.resume),
        'backend':'claude-code','transport':'native CLI; raw modelUsage and native turn-count metadata required; MCP adapter omits those fields','requested_model':'deepseek-v4-pro',
        'provider_host':urlsplit(env['ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL']).hostname,'settings_sources':'none; user-supplied key only in process env','tools':['Read','Grep','Glob']})
    with (trace/'stdout.json').open('w',encoding='utf-8') as out,(trace/'stderr.log').open('w',encoding='utf-8') as err:
        proc=subprocess.Popen(args,cwd=ROOT,env=env,stdin=subprocess.PIPE,stdout=out,stderr=err,text=True,encoding='utf-8')
        put(trace/'process.json',{'pid':proc.pid,'session_id':session});proc.communicate(prompt)
    put(trace/'exit.json',{'exit_code':proc.returncode,'utc':datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat()})
    if proc.returncode!=0:
        print(json.dumps({'status':'failed','exit_code':proc.returncode,'trace':str(trace)}));return
    raw=json.loads((trace/'stdout.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    if raw.get('is_error') or not raw.get('result'):
        print(json.dumps({'status':'error_or_empty','trace':str(trace)}));return
    (trace/'response.md').write_text(raw['result']+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    put(trace/'metadata.json',{'status':'completed','session_id':raw.get('session_id',session),'modelUsage':raw.get('modelUsage'),
        'usage':raw.get('usage'),'CLI_estimated_USD':raw.get('total_cost_usd'),'duration_ms':raw.get('duration_ms'),
        'turns':raw.get('num_turns'),'permission_denials':raw.get('permission_denials'),'provider_invoice':False,
        'identity_limit':'CLI reported service alias; no independent model-weight attestation'})
    print(json.dumps({'status':'completed','models':list((raw.get('modelUsage') or {}).keys()),'session_id':raw.get('session_id',session),'trace':str(trace)}))


if __name__=='__main__':main()

```

# Bounded representative selection at the assessment boundary

Prepared for execution under the user's existing R49 repair authorization and instruction to perform the full audit after generation. The initial authorization record truthfully states `audit_deferred_until_full_generation`, `review_completed=false`, and `scope_complete=false`; it is not a fabricated ready review. A complete actual code/result audit is still required before publication. No provider request, remote stage, launch, GPU call, or score computation was performed while preparing this change.

The latest attempt stopped after 210 committed questions and six completed Skill windows. Window 0006 has its completed trajectory/call evidence but no saved final repository or events. Two old analysis responses, `analysis_chexbench_slake_0081` and `analysis_chexbench_slake_0082`, each report skill `s_e1a0642fabefb0c7` twice. Both duplicates are `ADOPTED_POSITIVE`, but their step indices and evidence differ. The original strict assessment correctly rejects duplicate IDs. Taking the first or last entry mechanically would select evidence without justification; silently counting both would double count one skill's adoption.

For each known response, exactly one new namespaced META request asks the model to return only `{"selected_duplicate_index":0}` or `{"selected_duplicate_index":1}`. The index selects one of the two original duplicate entries. The model receives the same original completed trajectory, already released feedback and original image request, followed by its original response and a schema-only selection instruction. It supplies no new evidence or medical text. The selected entry is copied exactly, preserving its original status, step, and evidence. Every other unique entry is also preserved exactly and in original order. Invalid output or an unknown send stops; there is no second repair attempt.

This repair is applied only at the assessment boundary of window 0006 to a deep copy of its analyses. All old analyses still flow unchanged through the cached mutation/review/write phases. This is necessary because those cached request payloads already include the old analysis; changing it before writing would violate their immutable request binding. The original assessment implementation then performs all existing injected-ID, status, evidence, step-range, credit, baseline, and usage checks on the corrected copy. It counts a given skill at most once per trajectory. No completed prediction, context, feedback, earlier window, old analysis response, or old mutation request is modified.

The two fixed new namespaces are `analysis_chexbench_slake_0081_adoption_1` and `analysis_chexbench_slake_0082_adoption_1`, under the original META archive with ordinal 1. Their actual requests/replies/receipts remain preserved and counted once. Only after the original assessment succeeds is `failure_events/adoption_1_registration.json` written with schema `r49_registered_assessment_adoption_repair_v1`. It records original and effective adoption lists, model-selected indices, call paths, assess-only scope, and the unchanged write-phase analysis. It does not claim clinical correctness or causal Skill attribution.

For future new ordinary ANALYZE events, a static uniqueness clarification asks for at most one entry per injected skill and the best supported actual step. It adds no request and no clinical rule. Existing original or already clarified cached prompts replay exactly; unexpected saved prompt variants stop. Each dataset records `failure_events/analysis_unique_adoption_amendment.json` (`r49_analysis_unique_adoption_amendment_v1`) with its activation boundary and protected old-event list. Future invalid adoption lists still stop under the original assessment; the two-case repair is not an automatic repair loop.

The private helper is `local_assets/recover_cxr_online_skill_r49_adoption.py`, with `stage`, `launch`, and read-only `status` commands. Separate `SCOPE.json` and `EXECUTION_AUTHORIZATION.json` records are archived under `recovery/adoption_1`, alongside the prior raw exit-1/PID/log/queue evidence, new plan, controller/wrapper, amendments, and eventual registration. The new wrapper chains into `phrase_guard_1/resume_runner.py`, retaining all prior CUDA, META, field, embedding, and EOS-policy recovery behavior. The eight frozen R49 source files remain unchanged.

One new controller continues CheXbench, then SLAKE, then VQA-RAD serially, retaining existing GPU1 and disk checks. No additional tool or embedding retry is introduced. All old failed attempts remain failed in history. The final state `all_three_datasets_exited_zero_after_adoption_recovery` is valid only if the actual latest controller and all three original runners exit zero with complete evidence. Full-denominator scoring and the actual deferred audit follow complete generation, before publication; no target score or clinical Skill content was inspected to choose this technical repair.

### PRIMARY reports/cxr_online_skill_r49_20261108/deployment_source/PROVENANCE.md
```
# Executed-source and reporting provenance

This directory is an explicit publication copy assembled after all three R49 datasets completed. It preserves recorded deployed sources and reporting controls; it is not a byte, pixel, file, prediction, reference or prefix-hash attestation. No source hash was calculated for this copy. The existing runtime cache naming mechanism is separate from this provenance statement.

## Recorded source locations

- Remote project: `/home/imss/cw/MedRAX_reproduction_20261027`.
- Actual CODE: `/home/imss/cw/MedRAX_reproduction_20261027/code_cxr_online_skill_r49_20261108`.
- Actual RUN: `/home/imss/cw/MedRAX_reproduction_20261027/runs/online_skill_r49_20261108`.
- Local evidence mirror: `D:/MedRAX_Experiment_Artifacts/online_skill_r49_20261108`.

[Actual deployed sources](actual_code/) contain 249 payload files from the completed one-time source capture: regular immediate `experiments/*.py` files and `configs/cxr_vqa_tool_worker_gpu.json`. Source-capture started/completed receipts are preserved beside them. This includes the original R49 modules, existing runtime dependencies and the actually deployed `cxr_author_alignment.py` dependency. A source snapshot after execution does not establish the timing of every historical source file; the run protocol, original failures and operational recovery records provide the declared execution history.

The root RUN controller is preserved as [queue_controller.py](queue_controller.py), its executed protocol as [PROTOCOL.executed.json](PROTOCOL.executed.json), and its original execution-review marker as [EXECUTION_REVIEW_READY.json](EXECUTION_REVIEW_READY.json). This marker covers its original execution scope, not the later complete-results audit. Original deployed recovery controllers/wrappers and raw exit-1 history are supplied separately by the single shared recovery export. They must not be replaced with the final successful attempt alone.

Fifteen explicitly named local helper sources are copied flat in this directory. They cover the original deployment, individual dependency/tool/META/embedding/adoption continuations, isolated diagnostics, EOS policy implementation, completed-dataset pulls, once-only offline scoring and supplemental source preservation. These are sources that control transport/recovery/reporting; they are not all frozen Agent-method modules, and operational amendments remain disclosed in their individual plans and evidence.

## Completed-dataset reporting and canonical-input path failure

[reporting_provenance](reporting_provenance/) preserves the user-authorized reporting schedule, three dataset pull receipts, final-controls receipt, native scoring started/completed receipts and supplemental capture receipts. The first CheX scoring command stopped at the strict manifest/input semantic check before answer adaptation or reference scoring because the published data file used `assets/...` image aliases while the executed manifest used canonical `images/...` paths. The original started receipt, recorded pre-adaptation failure and explicitly authorized resume-start receipt are retained under [score_receipts](reporting_provenance/score_receipts/).

The actual canonical source `inputs.jsonl` was separately copied once from the recorded remote CheX data directory to the evidence mirror; both [that input file](reporting_provenance/dataset_completion/chexbench/source_data/inputs.jsonl) and its [source receipt](reporting_provenance/dataset_completion/chexbench/SOURCE_INPUT_PULL_RECEIPT.json) are preserved. This was an offline source-path correction; it did not alter R49 predictions, runtime or native public scoring rules. The completed CheX metric is reused from the successful native attempt. The historical accounting remains two native CLI attempts, one pre-adaptation failure and one completed metric calculation. SLAKE and VQA-RAD each retain their own independent once-only scoring records. No metric was recomputed by this source-copy step.

The canonical input JSONL records image paths; this provenance copy does not claim that its containing directory is a complete original-image export. The separate public evidence exporter must provide original/derived image assets and portable mappings.

## Supplemental failure evidence

The completed final-controls transfer preserved the full RUN recovery tree after all three actual child runners and the final controller exited zero. A separate local missing-only supplement then preserved the already captured CUDA 17-file diagnostic inventory, adoption 25-file failure-boundary inventory and technical metadata, and the phrase-token format mapping. Their original captures were not rerun. Existing status/exit files were left in place; missing original evidence was added without overwriting or content comparison. # Actual source line 114
def closed_calls(folder):
    calls = sorted((p for p in folder.iterdir() if p.is_dir()), key=lambda p: int(p.name)) if folder.exists() else []
    if [int(p.name) for p in calls] != list(range(1, len(calls)+1)):
        raise ValueError('noncontiguous_calls:' + str(folder))
    values = []
    for call in calls:
        if any(not (call/name).is_file() for name in ('request.json', 'started.json', 'response.json', 'result.json')):
            raise ValueError('unclosed_request:' + str(call))
        started, receipt = read(call/'started.json'), read(call/'result.json')
        if (receipt.get('error') or receipt.get('response_received') is not True
                or started.get('request_id') != receipt.get('request_id')
                or utc(started['utc']) > utc(receipt['utc'])):
            raise ValueError('unhealthy_request_receipt:' + str(call))
        values.append((call, started, receipt))
    return values

def registered_adoption_repair(run):
    """Two model choices of existing representatives, solely at assess boundary."""
    from run_cxr_action_r41 import parse
    from skill_prompts_r49 import ANALYZE
    from skill_services_r49 import Services
    path = run/'failure_events/adoption_1_registration.json'
    if set((run/'failure_events').glob('adoption_*_registration.json'))-{path}:
        raise ValueError('unrecognized_adoption_repair_registration')
    folders = run/'services/calls/R49-META'
    present = {folder.name for folder in folders.glob('*_adoption_*') if folder.is_dir()}
    if not path.is_file():
        if present:
            raise ValueError('unregistered_adoption_choice_namespace')
        return None
    value = read(path); manifest = read(run/'manifest.json')
    fixed = {'schema': 'r49_registered_assessment_adoption_repair_v1', 'recovery_id': 'adoption_1',
             'dataset': 'chexbench', 'arm': ARM, 'window_index': 6, 'activation_committed_questions': 210,
             'new_meta_requests': 2, 'assess_only': True, 'write_phase_analysis_unchanged': True,
             'other_analysis_fields_unchanged': True, 'automatic_retry': False,
             'status': 'two_assess_only_adoption_repairs_registered'}
    cases = value.get('repairs', [])
    if (manifest.get('dataset') != 'chexbench' or any(value.get(k) != v for k, v in fixed.items())
            or not isinstance(cases, list) or [case.get('source_question_id') for case in cases] != list(ADOPTION_SOURCES)):
        raise ValueError('only_two_fixed_assess_adoption_choices_supported')
    ordinals = {item['question_id']: index for index, item in enumerate(manifest['order'])}
    window = run/'windows/0006'; snapshot = read(window/'read_snapshot.json')
    credits = read(window/'events.json')['credit']; repairs = []
    for qid, case in zip(ADOPTION_SOURCES, cases):
        event = 'analysis_'+qid; repair_event = event+'_adoption_1'
        expected = {'source_event': event, 'repair_event': repair_event, 'source_question_id': qid,
                    'duplicate_skill_id': ADOPTION_SKILL,
                    'original_call_relative': 'services/calls/R49-META/'+event+'/1',
                    'repair_call_relative': 'services/calls/R49-META/'+repair_event+'/1',
                    'source_payload_unchanged': True, 'new_clinical_information': False}
        if any(case.get(k) != v for k, v in expected.items()) or ordinals.get(qid, -1)//30 != 6:
            raise ValueError('assess_repair_source_window_or_namespace_changed')
        old_calls, new_calls = closed_calls(folders/event), closed_calls(folders/repair_event)
        if len(old_calls) != 1 or len(new_calls) != 1:
            raise ValueError('one_closed_original_and_one_choice_call_per_known_analysis_required')
        original = parse(response_text(old_calls[0][0])); selected = parse(response_text(new_calls[0][0]))
        adoption = original.get('skill_adoption'); index = selected.get('selected_duplicate_index')
        if (set(selected) != {'selected_duplicate_index'} or type(index) is not int or index not in (0, 1)
                or not isinstance(adoption, list) or len(adoption) != 3):
            raise ValueError('fresh_reply_must_only_select_an_original_duplicate_index')
        positions = [i for i, item in enumerate(adoption) if item.get('skill_id') == ADOPTION_SKILL]
        entries = [adoption[i] for i in positions]
        if (len(entries) != 2 or entries[0] == entries[1]
                or any(item.get('status') != 'ADOPTED_POSITIVE' for item in entries)
                or {item.get('step_index') for item in entries} != {0, 1}):
            raise ValueError('known_different_evidence_positive_representatives_changed')
        effective = [item for i, item in enumerate(adoption) if item.get('skill_id') != ADOPTION_SKILL or i == positions[index]]
        if (case.get('original_adoption') != adoption or case.get('selected_duplicate_index') != index
                or case.get('effective_adoption') != effective):
            raise ValueError('effective_adoption_must_exactly_copy_model_selected_original_and_other_unique_entries')
        trajectory = read(window/'trajectories'/(qid+'.json'))
        validate_adoption_entries(effective, trajectory, snapshot)
        old_request = read(old_calls[0][0]/'request.json'); new_request = read(new_calls[0][0]/'request.json')
        messages = old_request.get('messages', [])
        if (len(messages) != 2 or messages[0] != {'role': 'system', 'content': ANALYZE}
                or messages[1].get('role') != 'user' or not isinstance(messages[1].get('content'), list)
                or not messages[1]['content'] or messages[1]['content'][0].get('type') != 'text'):
            raise ValueError('original_analysis_source_request_changed')
        payload = json.loads(messages[1]['content'][0]['text'])
        instruction = ADOPTION_SELECTION_INSTRUCTION+json.dumps({'duplicate_skill_id': ADOPTION_SKILL,
                                                               'duplicate_entries': entries}, ensure_ascii=False)
        expected_messages = copy.deepcopy(messages)+[{'role': 'assistant', 'content': response_text(old_calls[0][0])},
                                                     {'role': 'user', 'content': instruction}]
        if (payload.get('trajectory') != trajectory or trajectory.get('id') != qid
                or case.get('selection_instruction') != instruction
                or Services.text_semantics(new_request.get('messages')) != Services.text_semantics(expected_messages)
                or {k: v for k, v in old_request.items() if k != 'messages'} != {k: v for k, v in new_request.items() if k != 'messages'}
                or not old_calls[0][2].get('model_returned') or old_calls[0][2].get('model_returned') != new_calls[0][2].get('model_returned')
                or utc(new_calls[0][1]['utc']) < utc(old_calls[0][2]['utc'])):
            raise ValueError('adoption_choice_changed_original_source_model_or_added_information')
        mutation = folders/('mutation_'+qid)/'1/request.json'
        if mutation.is_file():
            mutation_payload = json.loads(read(mutation)['messages'][1]['content'][0]['text'])
            if mutation_payload.get('analysis') != original:
                raise ValueError('write_phase_cached_mutation_analysis_was_changed')
        actual = [(item.get('skill'), item.get('status')) for item in credits if item.get('trajectory') == qid]
        expected_credits = [(item['skill_id'], item['status']) for item in effective if item['status'] != 'IGNORED']
        if Counter(actual) != Counter(expected_credits):
            raise ValueError('registered_adoption_credit_must_be_once_per_representative')
        repairs.append({'source_event': event, 'repair_event': repair_event, 'original_call': old_calls[0],
                        'repair_call': new_calls[0], 'effective_adoption': effective,
                        'selected_duplicate_index': index, 'original_adoption': adoption})
    if present != {item['repair_event'] for item in repairs}:
        raise ValueError('unknown_or_extra_adoption_repair_namespace')
    return {'registration': value, 'repairs': repairs}

# Actual source line 402
def validate_analysis_unique_amendment(run, manifest=None):
    from skill_prompts_r49 import ANALYZE
    manifest = read(run/'manifest.json') if manifest is None else manifest
    path = run/'failure_events/analysis_unique_adoption_amendment.json'
    amendment = read(path) if path.is_file() else None; dataset = manifest.get('dataset')
    if amendment is not None:
        expected = {'schema': 'r49_analysis_unique_adoption_amendment_v1', 'recovery_id': 'adoption_1',
            'dataset': dataset, 'arm': ARM, 'activation_committed_questions': 210 if dataset == 'chexbench' else 0,
            'original_system': ANALYZE, 'clarification': ANALYSIS_UNIQUE_CLARIFICATION,
            'ordinary_event_rule': "system == original ANALYZE and event == 'analysis_' + payload['trajectory']['id']",
            'extra_ordinary_meta_calls': 0, 'automatic_future_repair': False, 'new_clinical_information': False, 'status': 'active'}
        protected = amendment.get('protected_original_events')
        if (dataset not in EXPECTED or any(amendment.get(k) != v for k, v in expected.items())
                or not isinstance(protected, list) or protected != sorted(set(protected))
                or dataset != 'chexbench' and protected):
            raise ValueError('only_fixed_analysis_uniqueness_prompt_boundary_supported')
        activation = utc(amendment['utc'])
    else:
        protected = []; activation = None
        if (run/'failure_events/adoption_1_registration.json').exists():
            raise ValueError('adoption_choice_requires_declared_analysis_prompt_boundary')
    repairs = {'analysis_'+qid+'_adoption_1' for qid in ADOPTION_SOURCES}
    observed = set(); original_count = clarified_count = 0
    indices = {item['question_id']: index for index, item in enumerate(manifest['order'])}
    size = manifest.get('defaults', {}).get('window', 30)
    for folder in sorted((run/'services/calls/R49-META').glob('analysis_*')):
        if not folder.is_dir() or folder.name in repairs:
            continue
        calls = closed_calls(folder)
        if len(calls) != 1:
            raise ValueError('one_closed_call_per_ordinary_analysis_required')
        messages = read(calls[0][0]/'request.json').get('messages', [])
        if (len(messages) != 2 or messages[0].get('role') != 'system' or messages[1].get('role') != 'user'
                or not isinstance(messages[1].get('content'), list) or not messages[1]['content']
                or messages[1]['content'][0].get('type') != 'text'):
            raise ValueError('ordinary_analysis_request_control_changed')
        payload = json.loads(messages[1]['content'][0]['text']); qid = payload.get('trajectory', {}).get('id')
        if folder.name != 'analysis_'+str(qid) or qid not in indices:
            raise ValueError('unknown_nonordinary_analysis_namespace')
        trajectory = read(run/'windows'/f'{indices[qid]//size:04d}'/'trajectories'/(qid+'.json'))
        if payload.get('trajectory') != trajectory:
            raise ValueError('ordinary_analysis_changed_saved_past_trajectory')
        system = messages[0]['content']
        if system == ANALYZE:
            original_count += 1; observed.add(folder.name)
            if amendment is not None and (folder.name not in protected or utc(calls[0][2]['utc']) > activation or indices[qid] >= 210):
                raise ValueError('new_or_unprotected_analysis_used_old_prompt')
        elif system == ANALYZE+ANALYSIS_UNIQUE_CLARIFICATION:
            clarified_count += 1
            if amendment is None or folder.name in protected or utc(calls[0][1]['utc']) < activation:
                raise ValueError('clarified_analysis_has_no_declared_activation')
        else:
            raise ValueError('unrecognized_ordinary_analysis_system_prompt')
    if amendment is not None and observed != set(protected):
        raise ValueError('protected_original_analysis_missing_or_reclassified')
    return {'active': amendment is not None, 'amendment': amendment,
            'ordinary_analysis_requests': original_count+clarified_count, 'original_system_requests': original_count,
            'clarified_system_requests': clarified_count, 'protected_original_events': protected, 'extra_ordinary_meta_calls': 0}

# Actual source line 910
def tool_attempt_accounting(run, tools, active_keys):
    registered = registered_cuda_retry(run)
    phrase = registered_phrase_guard_retry(run)
    registrations = [proof for proof in (registered, phrase) if proof]
    preserved = {proof['registration']['original_cache_key'] for proof in registrations}
    if registered and registered['registration']['retry_cache_key'] not in active_keys:
        raise ValueError('registered_healthy_retry_not_used_by_the_completed_stream')
    if registered:
        key = registered['registration']['retry_cache_key']
        expected = registered['registration']['retry_selection']['cuda_recovery']
        if any(t.get('cuda_recovery') != expected for t in tools if t.get('cache_key') == key):
            raise ValueError('future_registered_cache_alias_provenance_changed')
    if phrase and phrase['registration']['retry_cache_key'] not in active_keys:
        raise ValueError('registered_phrase_guard_retry_not_used_by_completed_stream')
    cache_root = run/'services/tool_cache'
    folders = [path for path in cache_root.iterdir() if path.is_dir()] if cache_root.is_dir() else []
    for folder in folders:
        if folder.name not in active_keys|preserved or any(not (folder/name).is_file() for name in ('started.json', 'result.json')):
            raise ValueError('orphan_or_unknown_tool_attempt')
    for key in active_keys:
        if any(not (run/'services/tool_cache'/key/name).is_file() for name in ('started.json', 'result.json')):
            raise ValueError('unclosed_active_tool_attempt')
    active = [read(run/'services/tool_cache'/key/'result.json') for key in sorted(active_keys)]
    if any(tool_failure_reason(value) for value in active):
        raise ValueError('failed_active_backend_attempt')
    physical = active + [proof['original_result'] for proof in registrations]
    return {'physical': physical, 'healthy': len(active), 'registered': registered, 'phrase': phrase}

# Actual source line 1027
def receipt_resources(values):
    records = [value[2] for value in values]
    known = [Decimal(str(value['settlement']['charged_cny'])) for value in records
             if value.get('settlement', {}).get('charged_cny') is not None]
    return {'requests': len(records), 'returned_models': sorted({v['model_returned'] for v in records if v.get('model_returned')}),
            'prompt_tokens': sum((v.get('usage') or {}).get('prompt_tokens', 0) or 0 for v in records),
            'completion_tokens': sum((v.get('usage') or {}).get('completion_tokens', 0) or 0 for v in records),
            'cached_tokens': sum(((v.get('usage') or {}).get('prompt_tokens_details') or {}).get('cached_tokens', 0) or 0 for v in records),
            'usage_cost_cny_proxy_known': str(sum(known, Decimal(0))), 'cost_unknown_count': len(records)-len(known),
            'elapsed_seconds_known': sum(v.get('elapsed_s') or 0 for v in records),
            'elapsed_unknown_count': sum(v.get('elapsed_s') is None for v in records), 'provider_invoice': False}

def resources(run, receipts, tools, attempts, meta_repair=None, meta_repair_2=None, fields_amendment=None,
              embedding_repair=None, phrase_amendment=None, adoption_repair=None, analysis_unique_amendment=None):
    if {p.name for p in (run/'services/calls').iterdir() if p.is_dir()}-{'R49-AGENT', 'R49-META'}:
        raise ValueError('unexpected_paid_call_arm')
    agent = receipt_resources([value for calls in receipts.values() for value in calls])
    meta = [value for folder in (run/'services/calls/R49-META').glob('*') if folder.is_dir() for value in closed_calls(folder)]
    embedding = []
    for folder in sorted((run/'services/embeddings').glob('*')):
        if not folder.is_dir():
            continue
        if embedding_repair is not None and folder.name == '000063':
            embedding.append(embedding_repair['original_failure']['result'])
            continue
        if any(not (folder/name).is_file() for name in ('request.json', 'started.json', 'response.json', 'result.json')):
            raise ValueError('unclosed_embedding_request')
        receipt = read(folder/'result.json')
        if receipt.get('error') or receipt.get('response_received') is not True:
            raise ValueError('failed_embedding_request')
        embedding.append(receipt)
    embeddings = {'requests': len(embedding), 'returned_models': sorted({r['model_returned'] for r in embedding if r.get('model_returned')}),
                  'successful_responses': sum(r.get('response_received') is True for r in embedding),
                  'registered_http_failed_attempts': int(embedding_repair is not None),
                  'usage_unknown_count': sum(r.get('usage') is None for r in embedding),

                  'usage_cost_cny_proxy_known': str(sum((Decimal(str(r['settlement']['charged_cny'])) for r in embedding if r.get('settlement', {}).get('charged_cny') is not None), Decimal(0))),
                  'cost_unknown_count': sum(r.get('settlement', {}).get('charged_cny') is None for r in embedding),
                  'elapsed_seconds_known': sum(r.get('elapsed_s') or 0 for r in embedding),
                  'elapsed_unknown_count': sum(r.get('elapsed_s') is None for r in embedding), 'provider_invoice': False}

                      'registered_phrase_failed_attempts': int(phrase is not None),
                      'failed_backend_attempts': int(registration is not None)+int(phrase is not None), 'unknown_backend_attempts': 0,
                      'backend_compute_seconds_known': sum(t.get('backend_compute_seconds') or 0 for t in physical),
                      'backend_compute_unknown_count': sum(t.get('backend_compute_seconds') is None for t in physical),
                      'backend_elapsed_seconds_known': sum(t.get('elapsed_s') or 0 for t in physical),
                      'backend_elapsed_unknown_count': sum(t.get('elapsed_s') is None for t in physical),
                      'failed_or_unknown_tools': sum(tool_failure_reason(t) is not None for t in tools)},
            'isolated_cuda_diagnostic': {
                'tool_invocations': diagnostic['tool_invocations'] if diagnostic else 0,
                'elapsed_s': diagnostic['elapsed_s'] if diagnostic else None,
                'main_api_calls': 0, 'meta_api_calls': 0, 'embedding_api_calls': 0,
                'result_reused_in_R49': False, 'included_in_production_attempts': False,
                'hardware_cost_unknown': diagnostic is not None},

                'generation_policy_changed': phrase is not None, 'same_generation_settings': False if phrase else None,
                'diagnostic_result_reused': False, 'included_in_tools_totals': True},
