```python
            'meta_schema_repair_2': meta_repair_resources(meta_repair_2),
            'adoption_repair': {'registered': adoption_repair is not None,
                'added_meta_requests': len(adoption_repair['repairs']) if adoption_repair else 0,
                'included_in_R49_META_totals': True, 'original_analysis_retained_for_mutation': True,
                'assess_only_overlay': True, 'model_selected_existing_entry_only': adoption_repair is not None,
                'manual_selection': False, 'new_evidence_written': False,

# Actual source line 1149
def score(data, run, references, dataset, *, r47_frozen=None, r47_run=None):
    n, baseline_name, expected_baseline = EXPECTED[dataset]
    inputs, manifest, paths = completed_gate(data, run, n)
    if manifest.get('dataset') != dataset:
        raise ValueError('dataset_manifest_identity_changed')
    current, predictions, receipts = adapt_current(inputs, run, paths)
    baseline = adapt_historical(inputs, dataset, baseline_name)
    comparisons = {baseline_name: baseline}
    if r47_run is not None or r47_frozen is not None:
        if dataset != 'chexbench':
            raise ValueError('R47_comparator_is_chexbench_only')
        comparisons['R47_online_GT'] = adapt_r47(inputs, baseline, r47_frozen, r47_run)
    # Reference-free adaptation of EVERY compared raw arm is now complete.
    hit, windows, tools, attempts = check_semantics(inputs, manifest, run, paths, predictions, receipts)
    meta_repairs = registered_meta_schema_repairs(run)
    fields_amendment = validate_mutation_fields_amendment(run, manifest)
    embedding_repair = registered_embedding_http_failure(run)
    phrase_amendment = validate_phrase_guard_amendment(run, manifest)
    adoption_repair = registered_adoption_repair(run)
    analysis_unique_amendment = validate_analysis_unique_amendment(run, manifest)
    resource = resources(run, receipts, tools, attempts, meta_repairs['meta_schema_1'],
                        meta_repairs['meta_schema_2'], fields_amendment, embedding_repair, phrase_amendment,
                        adoption_repair, analysis_unique_amendment)
    reference_rows = rows(references); refs = {row['question_id']: row['answer'] for row in reference_rows}
    if len(refs) != len(reference_rows) or set(refs) != {row['question_id'] for row in inputs}:
        raise ValueError('scoring_reference_cohort')
    detail, rng = [], random.Random(0)
    all_arms = {ARM: current, **comparisons}
    for row, info in zip(inputs, hit):
        qid = row['question_id']; event = read(paths['feedback'][qid])
        if event['reference_annotation']['answer'] != refs[qid]:
            raise ValueError('released_feedback_differs_from_scoring_reference')
        scores = {name: {**value[qid], **outcomes(row, value[qid], refs[qid], rng)} for name, value in all_arms.items()}
        detail.append({**info, 'subset': (row.get('source') or {}).get('subset', 'all'),
                       'reference': refs[qid], 'arms': scores})
    counts = {name: sum(d['arms'][name]['aligned_correct'] for d in detail) for name in all_arms}
    if counts[baseline_name] != expected_baseline or 'R47_online_GT' in counts and counts['R47_online_GT'] != 468:
        raise ValueError('fixed_historical_common_metric_count_changed')
    subsets = np.array([d['subset'] for d in detail]); clusters = cluster_keys(inputs)
    def summarize(name):
        groups = {label: {'n': sum(d['subset'] == label for d in detail),
                          'correct': sum(d['arms'][name]['aligned_correct'] for d in detail if d['subset'] == label)}
                  for label in sorted(set(subsets))}
        for group in groups.values():
            group['accuracy_pct'] = 100*group['correct']/group['n']
        return {'n': n, 'correct': counts[name], 'micro_pct': 100*counts[name]/n,
                'macro_pct': float(np.mean([g['accuracy_pct'] for g in groups.values()])), 'subsets': groups,
                'word_recall_pct': None if dataset == 'chexbench' else 100*float(np.mean([d['arms'][name]['word_recall'] for d in detail])),
                'answer_absent': sum(d['arms'][name]['answer_absent'] for d in detail),
                'domain_invalid': sum(d['arms'][name]['choice_domain_invalid'] for d in detail),
                'raw_whole_json_valid': sum(d['arms'][name]['raw_whole_json_valid'] for d in detail),
                'legacy_M_contract_compliant': sum(d['arms'][name]['legacy_M_contract_compliant'] for d in detail),
                'extraction_counts': dict(Counter(d['arms'][name]['extraction'] for d in detail))}
    paired = {}
    for name in comparisons:
        delta = np.array([int(d['arms'][ARM]['aligned_correct'])-int(d['arms'][name]['aligned_correct']) for d in detail])
        paired[name] = {'corrected': int(sum(delta > 0)), 'harmed': int(sum(delta < 0)), 'net': int(sum(delta)),
                        'micro_delta_pp': 100*float(delta.mean()),
                        'confidence': bootstrap(delta, clusters, subsets),
                        'changes': [{'question_id': d['question_id'], 'delta': int(value)} for d, value in zip(detail, delta) if value]}
    for window in windows:
        selected = [d for d in detail if d['window'] == window['window']]
        window['correct'] = sum(d['arms'][ARM]['aligned_correct'] for d in selected)
    summary = {'dataset': dataset, 'arm': ARM, 'questions': n, 'all_questions_fresh': True,
               'mode': 'online_post_prediction_ground_truth_feedback_and_window_skill_learning',
               'arms': {name: summarize(name) for name in all_arms}, 'comparisons': paired,
               'skill_hit_questions': sum(d['skill_hit'] for d in detail),
               'same_image_hit_questions': sum(d['same_image_hit'] for d in detail),
               'windows': windows, 'resources': resource, 'references_read_after_all_arms_adapted': True,
               'interpretation': ['Exposed online label-feedback system, not closed-test or official SOTA.',
                                  'Historical differences combine Skill learning, changed executor/call budgets, sampling and time.',
                                  'No contemporaneous no-Skill control; same-model adoption is not a causal or clinical verification.',
                                  'Datasets are reported separately; SLAKE overlap and VQA paraphrases preclude pooling independent cases.']}
    return summary, detail
```

```python
# Actual source line 130
def pull_dataset(client,dataset,resume_transfer=False):
    old=reused_dataset(dataset)
    if old is not None:return {'dataset':dataset,'reused_existing_complete_pull':True,'new_transfers':0,'receipt':str(receipt_path(dataset))}
    started=ARCHIVE/'dataset_completion'/dataset/'PULL_STARTED.json';started_path=safe_path(started)
    expected={'dataset':dataset,'remote_run':RUN,'archive':str(ARCHIVE),'questions':COUNTS[dataset]}
    if started_path.is_file():
        prior=json.loads(started_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        if not resume_transfer or any(prior.get(key)!=value for key,value in expected.items()):raise ValueError('partial_pull_requires_explicit_same_transfer_resume')
        if (SCORE_RECEIPTS/(dataset+'.json')).exists() or (SCORE_RECEIPTS/(dataset+'.started.json')).exists():
            raise ValueError('scored_or_started_scoring_dataset_may_not_be_repulled')
    elif safe_path(ARCHIVE/'generation'/dataset).exists():raise ValueError('existing_unreceipted_dataset_mirror_must_be_inspected')
    with client.open_sftp() as sftp:
        completion=complete_dataset(sftp,dataset)
        if not started_path.is_file():write_json(started,{**expected,'started_at':now(),'remote_generation_writes':False,'model_api_calls':0})
        inv=inventory();count=0
        try:
            count+=transfer_tree(sftp,RUN+'/generation/'+dataset,'generation/'+dataset,inv)
            for relative in ('jobs/'+dataset+'.exitcode','jobs/'+dataset+'.pid','jobs/'+dataset+'.'+RECOVERY+'.log',
                             'recovery/'+RECOVERY+'/'+dataset+'.exitcode','recovery/'+RECOVERY+'/'+dataset+'.pid'):
                count+=transfer_file(sftp,RUN+'/'+relative,relative,inv)
            # Complete GEN/failure_events supplies the scorer's repair proof.
            # Do not copy a whole recovery tree or the active controller log.
            write_json(ARCHIVE/'dataset_completion'/dataset/'control_snapshot.json',controller_snapshot(sftp))
        finally:write_json(ARCHIVE/'download_inventory.json',inv)
    receipt={**expected,**completion,'status':'complete_immutable_dataset_pulled','completed_at':now(),'files_transferred':count,
      'score_invoked':False,'references_parsed':False,'completion_marker_body_parsed':False,'other_dataset_predictions_pulled':False,'controller_may_still_run':True,
      'final_pull_must_preserve_this_generation_directory':True,'transfer_inventory_scope':'existing_size_mtime_resume_metadata_only_no_hashes'}
    write_json(receipt_path(dataset),receipt)
    return {'dataset':dataset,'reused_existing_complete_pull':False,'new_transfers':count,'questions':COUNTS[dataset],'receipt':str(receipt_path(dataset))}

def pull_final_controls(client,resume_transfer=False):
    receipt=ARCHIVE/'dataset_completion/FINAL_CONTROL_PULL_RECEIPT.json'
    if safe_path(receipt).is_file():return {'reused_existing_final_controls':True,'new_transfers':0}
    for dataset in DATASETS:
        if reused_dataset(dataset) is None:raise ValueError('pull_each_completed_dataset_before_final_controls:'+dataset)
    started=ARCHIVE/'dataset_completion/FINAL_CONTROL_PULL_STARTED.json'
    if safe_path(started).exists() and not resume_transfer:raise ValueError('partial_final_control_pull_requires_explicit_resume')
    with client.open_sftp() as sftp:
        if optional_remote(sftp,RUN+'/recovery/'+RECOVERY+'/controller.exitcode')!='0' or optional_remote(sftp,RUN+'/jobs/controller.exitcode')!='0':
            raise ValueError('final_control_pull_requires_actual_controller_exit_zero')
        queue=json_remote(sftp,RUN+'/jobs/queue_status.json')
        if (queue.get('state')!='all_three_datasets_exited_zero_after_adoption_recovery' or queue.get('recovery')!=RECOVERY
            or list(queue.get('datasets',[]))!=list(DATASETS) or queue.get('prior_production_attempts_include_exit1') is not True):
            raise ValueError('final_control_pull_requires_all_three_final_queue')
        for dataset in DATASETS:
            if optional_remote(sftp,RUN+'/jobs/'+dataset+'.exitcode')!='0' or optional_remote(sftp,RUN+'/recovery/'+RECOVERY+'/'+dataset+'.exitcode')!='0':
                raise ValueError('final_control_pull_requires_three_actual_child_exit_zero')
        if not safe_path(started).is_file():write_json(started,{'remote_run':RUN,'archive':str(ARCHIVE),'started_at':now(),'generation_directories_skipped':list(DATASETS)})
        inv=inventory();count=0
        try:
            for name in ('jobs','configs','recovery'):count+=transfer_tree(sftp,RUN+'/'+name,name,inv)
            for name in ('PROTOCOL.json','EXECUTION_REVIEW_READY.json','queue_controller.py'):
                if exists_remote(sftp,RUN+'/'+name):count+=transfer_file(sftp,RUN+'/'+name,name,inv)
        finally:write_json(ARCHIVE/'download_inventory.json',inv)
    write_json(receipt,{'status':'final_controls_pulled','remote_run':RUN,'archive':str(ARCHIVE),'completed_at':now(),'files_transferred':count,
      'generation_directories_skipped':list(DATASETS),'scores_invoked':False,'result_files_overwritten':False})
    return {'reused_existing_final_controls':False,'new_transfers':count,'generation_directories_skipped':list(DATASETS)}
```

```python
# Actual source line 81
def capture_source(client):
    fixed=binding();done=ACTUAL_CODE/'source_completed.json';started=ACTUAL_CODE/'source_started.json'
    if long(done).is_file():
        value=read(done)
        if value.get('binding')!=fixed or value.get('status')!='actual_code_source_copied':raise ValueError('source_capture_receipt_changed')
        return {'reused_completed_source_capture':True,'files_transferred':0}
    if long(started).exists() or long(ACTUAL_CODE).exists():raise ValueError('source_capture_started_or_unreceipted_inspect_without_automatic_repeat')
    with client.open_sftp() as sftp:
        remote=[]
        for entry in sftp.listdir_attr(CODE+'/experiments'):
            name=entry.filename
            if name.startswith('.') or '/' in name or '\\' in name or not name.endswith('.py') or not stat.S_ISREG(entry.st_mode):continue
            remote.append((CODE+'/experiments/'+name,'experiments/'+name))
        required=set(FILES)|{'cxr_author_alignment.py'}
        if not required <= {PurePosixPath(relative).name for _,relative in remote}:
            raise ValueError('actual_frozen_R49_modules_or_added_dependency_missing')
        config=CODE+'/configs/cxr_vqa_tool_worker_gpu.json'
        if not stat.S_ISREG(sftp.stat(config).st_mode):raise ValueError('actual_worker_gpu_config_not_regular')
        remote.append((config,'configs/cxr_vqa_tool_worker_gpu.json'))
        exclusive_json(started,{'binding':fixed,'started_at':now(),'scope':'immediate_python_sources_and_one_known_config_only',
          'remote_execution':False,'model_api_calls':0,'hash_validation':False,'shared_download_inventory_used':False})
        records=[]
        for source,relative in sorted(remote):
            target=child(ACTUAL_CODE,relative)
            if target.exists():raise ValueError('actual_source_target_already_exists_no_overwrite')
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);part=target.with_name(target.name+'.download_part')
            sftp.get(source,str(part),prefetch=False)
            os.link(part,target);part.unlink()
            records.append({'remote':source,'local':relative})
    exclusive_json(done,{'binding':fixed,'status':'actual_code_source_copied','completed_at':now(),'files':records,
      'remote_execution':False,'model_api_calls':0,'byte_or_pixel_or_hash_checks':False,'shared_download_inventory_used':False,
      'scope':'actual deployed Python source snapshot, no source byte attestation'})
    return {'reused_completed_source_capture':False,'files_transferred':len(records),'receipt':str(done)}

def preserve_local_captures():
    fixed=binding();done=RECEIPTS/'local_captures_completed.json';started=RECEIPTS/'local_captures_started.json'
    if long(done).is_file():
        value=read(done)
        if value.get('binding')!=fixed or value.get('status')!='known_local_captures_preserved':raise ValueError('local_capture_receipt_changed')
        return {'reused_completed_local_capture':True,'files_copied':0}
    if long(started).exists():raise ValueError('local_capture_started_inspect_without_automatic_repeat')
    final_path=ARCHIVE/'dataset_completion/FINAL_CONTROL_PULL_RECEIPT.json'
    if not long(final_path).is_file():raise ValueError('actual_final_controls_receipt_required_before_local_supplement')
    final=read(final_path)
    if final.get('status')!='final_controls_pulled' or final.get('remote_run')!=RUN or final.get('archive')!=str(ARCHIVE):
        raise ValueError('actual_final_controls_receipt_required_before_local_supplement')
    fixed,records=local_capture_map()
    if any(not long(source).is_file() for source,_ in records):raise ValueError('known_local_capture_file_missing_no_remote_recapture')
    exclusive_json(started,{'binding':fixed,'started_at':now(),'scope':'known_local_inventory_records_missing_only','remote_requests':0,
      'generation_writes':False,'existing_files_overwritten':False})
    copied=[];preserved=[]
    for source,target in records:
        item={'source':str(normal(source)),'destination':str(normal(target).relative_to(ARCHIVE.resolve()))}
        (copied if copy_missing(source,target) else preserved).append(item)
    exclusive_json(done,{'binding':fixed,'status':'known_local_captures_preserved','completed_at':now(),'copied_missing':copied,
      'existing_preserved_without_reading_or_overwriting':preserved,'remote_requests':0,'generation_writes':False,
      'capture_or_diagnostic_reexecuted':False,'hashes_computed':False})
    return {'reused_completed_local_capture':False,'files_copied':len(copied),'existing_preserved':len(preserved),'receipt':str(done)}
```

```python
# Actual source line 104
def actual_review(raw_path, metadata_path, claims_path):
    """Require a real completed result review, allowing any positive/negative verdict."""
    raw_path, metadata_path, claims_path = map(Path, (raw_path, metadata_path, claims_path))
    paths = {name: in_report(path) for name, path in
             [('raw', raw_path), ('metadata', metadata_path), ('claims', claims_path)]}
    metadata = read(metadata_path)
    require(metadata.get('status') == 'completed' and not metadata.get('is_error')
            and metadata.get('session_id') and metadata.get('duration_ms', 0) > 0
            and metadata.get('turns', 0) > 0 and metadata.get('modelUsage')
            and any('deepseek' in model.lower() for model in metadata['modelUsage'])
            and len(raw_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').strip()) > 20
            and len(claims_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').strip()) > 20,
            'actual_user_selected_DeepSeek_result_review_required')
    # No requirement for a favorable verdict. An execution-only ready score
    # cannot stand in for this separately named result-review artifact.
    return dict(paths=paths, metadata=metadata, identity_assurance='CLI-reported service alias, not weight attestation',
                clinical_or_integrity_audit_claim=False, favorable_verdict_required=False)

# Actual source line 123
def publication_sequence():
    """Keep the user's later evidence-first order separate from original plans."""
    history = read(REPORT/'PUBLICATION_HISTORY.json')
    first = history['first_evidence_publication']
    committed = read(ROOT/'local_assets/r49_evidence_first_publication/commit.json')
    pushed = read(ROOT/'local_assets/r49_evidence_first_publication/push_completed.json')
    require(first.get('status') == 'git_push_completed_exit0'
            and first.get('commit') == committed.get('commit') == pushed.get('commit')
            and pushed.get('exit_code') == 0
            and first.get('final_result_review_completed_at_first_push') is False
            and history['user_sequence_amendment'].get('first_upload_before_final_review') is True
            and history['post_push_remote_verification'].get('waived_by_user') is True
            and history['post_push_remote_verification'].get('performed') is False,
            'actual_user_directed_first_push_and_post_verification_waiver_required')
    return {'history': 'PUBLICATION_HISTORY.json', 'amendment': 'PUBLICATION_SEQUENCE_AMENDMENT.md',
            'first_evidence_publication': first,
            'first_publication_preceded_final_result_review': True,
            'final_review_timing': 'after_generation_and_first_evidence_push_before_final_report_followup',
            'original_deferred_prelaunch_control_unchanged': True,
            'post_push_remote_verification_waived_by_user': True,
            'post_push_remote_verification_performed': False}

# Actual source line 362
def offline_scoring_provenance(archive, preparation, receipt_root, public_root, bundles):
    """Bind already saved scores to their receipts; never invoke a scorer.

    Public files are explicit root-prepared copies of known control records.
    Their JSON values are compared semantically, without file/image digests.
    No input, reference, Skill, response or answer is extracted here.
    """
    archive, receipt_root, preparation = map(Path, (archive, receipt_root, preparation))
    public_root = Path(public_root).resolve()
    require(public_root.is_relative_to(REPORT.resolve()) and public_root.is_dir(), 'actual_public_reporting_provenance_directory_required')
    files = []

    def paired(source, relative):
        source, public = Path(source), public_root/relative
        value = read(source)
        require(read(public) == value, 'published_reporting_receipt_must_match_actual_control_record:'+relative)
        files.append({'private_source': str(source.resolve()), 'public': in_report(public)})
        return value

    authorization = paired(preparation/'DATASET_COMPLETION_REPORTING_AUTHORIZATION.json', 'DATASET_COMPLETION_REPORTING_AUTHORIZATION.json')
    require(authorization.get('enabled') is True and authorization.get('confirmation_source') == 'explicit_user_reply'
            and authorization.get('exact_user_reply') == '848题是这个三个数据集吧，可以做完一个数据集汇报一个数据集的准确率等成果'
            and authorization.get('datasets') == {ds: SPEC[ds]['n'] for ds in DATASETS}
            and authorization.get('report_on_each_dataset_completion') is True
            and authorization.get('partial_dataset_scoring_allowed') is False
            and authorization.get('reuse_saved_dataset_scores_at_finalization') is True
            and authorization.get('future_inference_tuned_from_report') is False
            and authorization.get('final_all_three_exit_zero_gate_unchanged') is True,
            'actual_completed_dataset_reporting_authorization_without_future_tuning_required')
    records = {}
    for ds in DATASETS:
        receipt = paired(receipt_root/(ds+'.json'), 'score_receipts/'+ds+'.json')
        started = paired(receipt_root/(ds+'.started.json'), 'score_receipts/'+ds+'.started.json')
        identity = receipt.get('identity', {}); n = SPEC[ds]['n']; failed = int(ds == 'chexbench')
        require(receipt.get('schema') == 'r49_completed_dataset_score_receipt_v1' and receipt.get('status') == 'completed'
                and receipt.get('native_score_invocations') == 1+failed
                and receipt.get('completed_metric_calculations') == 1 and receipt.get('pre_adaptation_failed_invocations') == failed
                and receipt.get('new_inference_calls') == 0 and receipt.get('references_read_after_all_arms_adapted') is True
                and receipt.get('later_dataset_policy_frozen_do_not_tune_from_interim_scores') is True
                and receipt.get('summary_snapshot') == bundles[ds]['summary']
                and receipt.get('scored_question_ids') == [row['question_id'] for row in bundles[ds]['details']]
                and receipt.get('questions') == n and identity.get('dataset') == ds,
                'saved_completed_metric_must_match_exact_once_receipt_not_be_recomputed:'+ds)
        expected_paths = {'run': archive/'generation'/ds, 'jobs': archive/'jobs',
                          'runner_exit': archive/'recovery/adoption_1'/(ds+'.exitcode'), 'out': REPORT/'results'/ds}
        require(all(Path(identity[key]).resolve() == path.resolve() for key, path in expected_paths.items()),
                'score_receipt_must_bind_final_immutable_mirror_and_existing_output:'+ds)
        exits = receipt.get('actual_raw_exit_records', [])
        expected_exits = {str(path.resolve()) for path in (archive/'generation'/ds/'generation.exitcode',
                          archive/'jobs'/(ds+'.exitcode'), archive/'recovery/adoption_1'/(ds+'.exitcode'))}
        require(len(exits) == 3 and {str(Path(item['path']).resolve()) for item in exits} == expected_exits

# Actual source line 492
def recorded_scoring_sources(actual, provenance):
    """Report the real source paths, including CheX's separate references file."""
    actual = dict(actual)
    for ds in DATASETS:
        identity = provenance['datasets'][ds]['identity']
        for field, identity_key in (('data', 'data'), ('references', 'references')):
            key = ds+'_'+field; recorded = identity[identity_key]
            require(actual.get(key) is None or Path(actual[key]).resolve() == Path(recorded).resolve(),
                    'explicit_report_source_must_match_completed_score_receipt:'+key)
            actual[key] = recorded
    return actual

def make_readme(bundles, extras, resource, review):
    sentences = '\n\n'.join(sentence(ds, bundles[ds]['summary']) for ds in DATASETS)
    tool_rows = [[SPEC[ds]['label'], (r := bundles[ds]['summary']['resources']['tools'])['logical_calls'], r['cache_hits'],
                  r['fresh_backend_attempts'], r['healthy_backend_attempts'], r['registered_failed_historical_attempts'],
                  num(r['backend_compute_seconds_known']), extras[ds]['fresh_tool_backend_time_unknown_count'], r['failed_or_unknown_tools'],
                  num(extras[ds]['recorded_request_span_seconds'])] for ds in DATASETS]
    m = review['metadata']; probe_value = resource['separate_embedding_probe']; repair = resource['dependency_recovery']; cuda = resource['cuda_recovery']; schema = resource['meta_schema_recovery']; fields = resource['mutation_fields_recovery']; embed = resource['embedding_http_recovery']; phrase = resource['phrase_guard_recovery']; adoption = resource['adoption_recovery']; scoring_provenance = resource['offline_scoring_provenance']; publication = resource['publication_sequence']
    variants = [[SPEC[ds]['label'], (v := fields['per_dataset_prompt_amendments'][ds])['ordinary_mutation_requests'],
                 v['original_system_requests'], v['clarified_system_requests'], len(v['protected_original_events']),
                 v['extra_ordinary_meta_calls']] for ds in DATASETS]
    embedding_rows = [[SPEC[ds]['label'], (e := bundles[ds]['summary']['resources']['embedding'])['requests'],
                       e['successful_responses'], e['registered_http_failed_attempts'], e['usage_unknown_count'],
                       e['total_tokens'], e['cost_unknown_count']] for ds in DATASETS]
    phrase_rows = [[SPEC[ds]['label'], (p := phrase['per_dataset_policy_amendments'][ds])['amendment']['activation_committed_questions'],
                    p['legacy_saved_selections'], p['legacy_healthy_cache_selections'], p['guarded_new_selections'],
                    p['guarded_cache_selections'], p['guarded_physical_attempts'], p['masked_eos_steps']] for ds in DATASETS]
    analysis_rows = [[SPEC[ds]['label'], (a := adoption['per_dataset_analysis_amendments'][ds])['amendment']['activation_committed_questions'],
                      a['ordinary_analysis_requests'], a['original_system_requests'], a['clarified_system_requests'],
                      len(a['protected_original_events']), a['extra_ordinary_meta_calls']] for ds in DATASETS]
    sections = '\n\n'.join(make_dataset(ds, bundles[ds], extras[ds]) for ds in DATASETS)
    score_attempt_rows = [[SPEC[ds]['label'], (s := scoring_provenance['datasets'][ds])['native_score_invocations'],
                           s['completed_metric_calculations'], s['pre_adaptation_failed_invocations'],
                           link('原始凭证', s['score_receipt'])] for ds in DATASETS]
    return f'''# R49：同图真值反馈＋在线窗口Skill，全量三清单结果

{sentences}

以上保留实际正负结果，无增分也不改变运行记录。三份清单分别报告：618、114、116，共848个新生成题目级记录，不合成848个独立病例的总体准确率。最终原runner的三组实际原始退出均为0，最后一次adoption_1接续controller也退出0；首次依赖故障、后续CUDA、两次元学习schema、Embedding、MAIRA及采用归因校验中断的原始退出1与日志全部保留，不能说所有尝试都退出0。冻结运行源文件未改，但下文列明操作wrapper、提示、故障闭合例外、MAIRA生成策略及采用归因接口修订；完整生成后统一答案口径评分、导出和公开语义重算，没有靠评分解析放宽或历史答案填充获得结果。

## 已完成组的离线评分与来源

用户要求每组完整结束后先报告该组结果，后续数据集照冻结方案继续。各组summary／per_question已保存后在此原样复用，没有第二次私有指标计算或据此改后续推理。CheX首次进入原生评分函数在输入路径门槛失败：公开别名`assets/...`与真实运行清单的`images/...`不一致，发生于ALL-arm适配、参考／feedback／complete正文读取之前，未产生分数。保存原始started／退出1故障元数据后，只复制一次实际canonical源inputs并显式接续同一已知前置失败；原生gate、解析和指标不改，预测不改，模型调用0。故函数进入次数与完整指标计算次数分别报告，不能把首个失败称另一套已完成成绩。

## 信息条件与方法

唯一新方法`{ARM}`，所有题新生成，各数据集空Skill／空同图记忆冷启动。主与元调用qwen3.7-plus，.2／.95／2048／thinking=false；主／工具调用次数无固定上限，以合法终答、三次重复无进展、技术异常终止。窗口30、buffer20、三分支top6、每两窗口治理。过去同图原模型答案／理由／健康工具证据保持原样，预测及保存事件完成后才揭示本题标注，供后续同图题和窗口学习。没有当前／未来真值或其他数据集的Skill／病例传入。

R49为R47式在线记忆与SkeMex启发的项目适配，非官方SkeMex、Evo-MedAgent或历史M43执行器的完整复现。解除了旧预算并改变循环，没有同期无Skill对照。历史记录、信息优势、调用资源、采样／时段共同变化，差值不能单独归因Skill；同模型Analysis／Review和采用信用不证明临床正确或因果归因。[方案](PLAN.md) · [实际协议](PROTOCOL.json) · [来源与限制](METHOD_AND_LIMITS.md)。

CheX保留488图组／130后续题，五跨子集同图组十题；另列SLAKE30图组、VQA58图组。SLAKE114与CheX-SLAKE123同源共享17图、64相同图像＋措辞题，114中73题位于共享图像；没有论文精确114题号核验。VQA同图复述按图聚簇。三清单已有反复开发暴露，不称未见测试、闭卷SOTA、同预算因果收益或稳定医学泛化。原无在线反馈最好完整记录466／108／100仍按原条件单列，R47在线GT468/618也保持独立记录。

## 审阅、故障与复现边界

首次controller2277353／CheX进程2277359在第一题`{repair['first_question']}`的预测、保存事件及自身反馈均完成后，于已完成题的学习奖励适配处报`ModuleNotFoundError: cxr_author_alignment`，原始退出1。此时只有第一题，3次主请求全部闭合、2次工具健康，尚无元学习／Embedding请求或第二题。原因是部署只复制了推理依赖，缺少在线奖励使用的既有答案适配模块；按原样补齐该模块，未修改runner、Skill规则、模型、配置、题序、标签或已保存输出。独立窄范围DeepSeek接续审阅就绪后使用单次守卫恢复，同一输出目录读取第一题既有预测／事件／反馈，构建其轨迹再继续，不重新作答或重复读取第一题标注。该次依赖接续后来又因下面的CUDA问题退出1，作为中间失败保留；最终原始退出0来自后续登记操作恢复，不是把依赖接续本身改记成功。

[依赖故障与恢复说明]({repair['review_files']['explanation']}) · [实际接续审阅原文]({repair['review_files']['raw']}) · [接续审阅元数据]({repair['review_files']['metadata']}) · [完整恢复树]({repair['public_tree']}) · [首次退出1／日志／原manifest]({repair['public_tree']}/original)。恢复后各组仍由原冻结runner完整运行至实际原始退出0，没有effective exit、自动技术空答或吞任意异常；首次失败不被改记成功。第一题3次主请求费用代理已知小计{repair['first_question_main_usage_cost_cny_known']}CNY、费用未知{repair['first_question_main_cost_unknown_count']}条，已知主请求时间合计{num(repair['first_question_main_elapsed_seconds_known'])}秒、时间未知{repair['first_question_main_elapsed_unknown_count']}条；它们和2次工具均已包含在CheX资源总量中一次，不能再加算。窄范围接续审阅实际{repair['review_metadata']['duration_ms']}ms、CLI估算USD={repair['review_metadata'].get('CLI_estimated_USD')}，与主模型CNY分列，不冒充发票。失败前后完整墙钟及部署修复墙钟若没有独立时间记录则保持未知；跨中断的请求时段仍按其实际跨度披露。

随后已完成前{cuda['preserved_completed_prefix_questions']}题和一个窗口，第{cuda['question_ordinal']}题`{cuda['question_id']}`在原主计划回复后，expert工具产生已关闭的`CUDA error: device kernel image is invalid`失败，当前题尚未保存预测或揭示自身真值。一次隔离debug诊断健康，但结果仅用于诊断，未写入生产cache或作为推理证据。实际DeepSeek操作审阅后，针对这一确定失败进行了恰好一次原始生产参数复试，关闭debug环境标志；仅复试健康后登记`r49_registered_cuda_retry_v1`并将当前工具槽位解析到`<oldkey>_cuda_1`健康cache。原坏cache和原选择归档保持不变，未来只有同后端／revision／原图路径／参数的精确登记身份可复用该健康cache。冻结八份推理源文件未改，但一次生产复试和登记cache解析覆盖属于明确的操作协议修订，不应说执行完全没有变化。

[CUDA操作修订说明]({cuda['review_files']['explanation']}) · [实际操作审阅]({cuda['review_files']['raw']}) · [元数据]({cuda['review_files']['metadata']}) · [操作helper源码]({cuda['review_files']['helper']}) · [完整恢复树／原始失败与诊断]({cuda['public_tree']}) · [GEN登记](evidence/chexbench/failure_events/cuda_1_registration.json) · [原失败选择归档](evidence/chexbench/failure_events/cuda_1_original_selection.json)。操作审阅实际{cuda['review_metadata']['duration_ms']}ms、CLI估算USD={cuda['review_metadata'].get('CLI_estimated_USD')}，单独报告。前40个预测不重生成，第41题既有主计划只复用已保存的第一条回复，不重发；该主请求费用代理{cuda['known_q41_plan_usage_cost_cny_proxy']}CNY、耗时{num(cuda['known_q41_plan_elapsed_seconds'])}秒，若缺失则未知，均已在主模型总量中一次计入。工具失败与复试的已知计算／经过时间及未知项沿实际资源原字段保留，隔离诊断另列。debug成功或生产复试健康均不能确定根因，更不能声称CUDA问题永久修复。未知发送、其他失败和新增错误不在这一登记例外内；没有自动技术空答或未登记重试。

CUDA接续在第4个窗口、120个已保存预测与反馈之后再次停止：`{schema['source_event']}`返回PATCH，但`patch_section="c"`表示整个内容容器，不在原六个允许的单字段集合内，冻结进化代码因此报`ValueError: invalid_patch_section`。前120题、前三个已完成窗口及此前CUDA登记仍保留，尚未产生第121题上下文或作答。此处旧回复已收到、付费凭证闭合，属于已知元学习schema失败，不是未知发送，也不是模型题目答案被判错后重答。

经实际窄范围DeepSeek审阅后，只为这一已知事件新增一次独立META请求`{schema['repair_event']}`：使用完全相同的已完成历史payload，追加schema说明，没有新病例、当前／未来真值或额外临床信息；PATCH只能保留同一目标并选择原六个合法字段，也允许模型依据原证据真实返回NONE，不由操作方把无效响应强制改成NONE。本次实际新控制输出为{json.dumps(schema['repair_control'], ensure_ascii=False)}。冻结evolve的合法性验证／质量审查不改，只有这一事件的服务路由增加明确登记的schema澄清；随后仍接受原Review，不借此跳过后续失败。

[META恢复方案与边界]({schema['review_files']['explanation']}) · [真实操作审阅]({schema['review_files']['raw']}) · [元数据]({schema['review_files']['metadata']}) · [helper源码]({schema['review_files']['helper']}) · [完整恢复树／原始失败]({schema['public_tree']}) · [GEN登记](evidence/chexbench/failure_events/meta_schema_1_registration.json)。实际操作审阅{schema['review_metadata']['duration_ms']}ms、CLI估算USD={schema['review_metadata'].get('CLI_estimated_USD')}，与主／元模型CNY分列。原无效回复和新回复的实际token／费用／耗时分别见RESOURCE_SUMMARY的`non_additive_META_cost_subsets`；二者都已包含在R49_META总账一次。额外主Agent／Embedding／医学工具调用均0，不重新生成120题、重复揭示其标签或重跑前三个窗口；原CUDA cache覆盖继续生效，未再次运行CUDA生产复试。该次meta_schema_1接续后来在150题后的第五窗口再次因下述schema失配退出1，也作为中间失败保留；最终完整0不来自把这一尝试改记成功。学习接口格式恢复不算医学正确率增益，不能据此声称改进稳定性或临床能力已证明。

在150个已提交预测／反馈、四个完整窗口之后，`{fields['source_event']}`再次给出PATCH，但字段为`content`而不是原六个允许字段，冻结校验仍以`invalid_patch_section`停止。实际操作审阅通过后，仅为这一已知、已闭合事件独立新增一次同历史payload、同一目标的schema澄清请求`{fields['repair_event']}`；实际新控制为{json.dumps(fields['repair_control'], ensure_ascii=False)}，只能是合法单字段PATCH或模型真实NONE，不能操作方强行NONE。原无效回复、两份请求回复及两份费用均保留；不重生成150题、重读其标签或重跑前四个已提交窗口，不重新执行此前CUDA生产复试。

这次操作还在三个数据集的普通MUTATE边界明确原六字段，禁止把`c`／`content`整个容器当字段。仅当事件名精确等于`mutation_`＋既有payload轨迹题号且原system为MUTATE时应用；原payload、模型参数和临床规则不变。已缓存的原提示变体按冻结保护清单回放，后续未缓存普通事件只增加固定字段说明，没有额外调用。CheX在150题处激活，另列SLAKE和VQA从各自冷启动时激活；不是把CheX Skill库传给后两组。任何后续新无效输出仍然停止，没有自动修复循环。这是明确的运行中操作提示变更，不能再称整个流自始至终遵循同一原始静态提示，收益归因更受混杂限制。

上述字段修订实际取得第二次合法PATCH回复后，在同一150题边界的Embedding000063又因SDK `BadRequestError`停止，fields2 controller／CheX原始退出1也保留。原记录没有response.json、HTTPbody或usage；400仅来自已安装SDK异常类别的状态码约定。后来一次中性诊断返回400／Arrearage，只能证明该次诊断时账户欠费，不能把它的body冒充原000063响应或据此重建原请求的具体服务器原因。

用户明确确认DashScope充值且实际新操作审阅就绪后，仅为原000063相同input／model／端点，以安装SDK的原缺省参数新增一次生产Embedding请求000063_arrearage_1。没有修改文本、模型、encoding_format参数或临床规则；收到真实有限1024维健康向量后，只为登记的精确身份建立别名，诊断结果从未用作向量。原失败请求／started／result和缺失body保持原样；不制造旧response.json。启动及最终闭合校验也只有这一份明确登记、已封存的SDK400失败例外，其它未知发送／失败仍立即停止，不能说原来的所有闭合规则完全未变。150个预测、前四个完整repo和此前CUDA／两次META包装器均保留；生产Agent／META／医学工具没有因这次修复新增调用。

## MAIRA未闭合格式与显式生成策略修订

第169题`{phrase['question_id']}`的原MAIRA生产调用已明确失败。独立诊断在原设置下实际生成13token即EOS，生成后缀含`<obj>`而没有`</obj>`；两者在原tokenizer中都不是special，结束标签未曾生成，不能归因于decode过滤。13token未达到原max300上限，原processor严格断言拒绝。诊断用于定位这一机制，不能补标签、补box、猜阴性，不能当作同图医学观察，也未复用其输出。

实际新操作审阅通过后，`phrase_guard_1`对CheX第168个已提交预测、5个完整窗口之后的全部**新物理MAIRA调用**统一启用EOS守卫；另列SLAKE和VQA从各自冷启动启用。只根据新生成后缀的obj／box未闭合状态屏蔽EOS，其余logit不改，原input／model／量化／主prompt／max300与严格原parser保持；不强制生成标签、box、发现或阴性，不作解析回退。若守卫后仍未闭合、未正常EOS或原parser拒绝，继续技术停止，没有自动重试循环。第169题仅一次新guarded生产尝试，旧失败cache／旧selection与其原始耗时完整保留；此前168题及五个完整窗口、该题已保存主调用均没有重做，所有先前恢复包装器继续保留。

旧健康cache和已有选择保持legacy policy，不改写为新策略产物。新策略仅覆盖激活后的新物理调用，因而全流混合旧／新生成策略；本次是**自定义generation-policy amendment**，不是官方MAIRA修复，也不是与原设置相同的重试。EOS屏蔽可能改变后续文本、坐标、调用耗时和正确性，不能声称语义不受影响、医学能力获得独立验证，或把在线反馈／Skill／预算／提示／生成策略混合变化的差值作单一因果归因。

## 两次采用归因代表项选择与完成后审计

窗口0006的两个原ANALYZE事件`analysis_chexbench_slake_0081`／`analysis_chexbench_slake_0082`，分别为同一个已注入Skill给出了两条同为ADOPTED_POSITIVE、但step_index和evidence不同的采用项。这些不是完全相同的重复记录；原assess的唯一Skill约束以`duplicate_skill_adoption`停止，尚无第211题，也没有该窗口已提交repo／events。原回复、请求、费用、210题及前六个完整窗口保持。

只对这两个已闭合已知事件，各新增一次独立META请求。模型只能返回`selected_duplicate_index`为0或1，选择该事件原来的两个重复项之一；不是操作方择优，也不重写任何status、step或evidence。实际选择为{json.dumps(adoption['model_chosen_original_entry_indices'], ensure_ascii=False)}。effective adoption只留下模型选中的完整原项，其余唯一项原样保持；该有效列表仅在原assess边界使用。原ANALYZE原文及MUTATE使用的分析保持，未重跑原窗口的生成／标签揭示或已缓存普通ANALYZE／MUTATE调用。两次新请求与两份原付费回复均已在R49_META总账一次计入，RESOURCE_SUMMARY的adoption_recovery.non_additive_META_cost_subsets仅作拆分、不再相加；无额外主Agent／Embedding／工具调用。

对后续未缓存普通ANALYZE，只在原system／精确事件名边界增加“每个注入skill_id最多一个采用项”的固定说明，原payload和模型配置保留，每次普通事件仍只有一次调用。旧缓存原提示按声明保护清单回放。CheX在210题处激活，另列SLAKE／VQA各自空库冷启动时激活，记忆及Skill仍逐数据集隔离。后续新的不合法采用分析继续停止，不形成自动修复循环。此操作会改变采用证据代表项、信用分配及后续Skill效用轨迹，不能称输出语义不变，或把差值单独归因记忆／Skill的医学能力。

{table(['数据集', '激活前已提交题数', '普通ANALYZE请求', '原system请求', '澄清system请求', '保护原缓存事件数', '普通澄清额外调用'], analysis_rows)}

用户最初明确指示“这个可以跑完再审计？”。因此初始`EXECUTION_AUTHORIZATION.json`如实保留`audit_deferred_until_full_generation`、review_completed=false、scope_complete=false、explicit_user_reply及原定完成后发布前审计要求；不将其改写为曾经预审ready。随后用户明确要求“先上传github吧”“抓紧推送”，因此先在`{publication['first_evidence_publication']['commit']}`公开完整已完成结果与证据，当时最终结果审阅尚未完成。本页对应后续最终报告：同一次实际DeepSeek审阅发生在全部848题及首次证据推送之后、最终报告补充推送之前，覆盖adoption_protocol、request_resource_accounting、complete_results_and_claims，scope_complete来自真实结论。不能再声称审阅发生在第一次发布之前；最新用户指示和原始历史授权分开保存。这里与下文RESULT_CLAIM审阅是同一次，不重复计费，负结果可以如实发布。[发布顺序修订](PUBLICATION_SEQUENCE_AMENDMENT.md)与[实际发布控制记录](PUBLICATION_HISTORY.json)保留这一顺序。

[采用项恢复方案]({adoption['review_files']['explanation']}) · [实际完成后的统一审计]({adoption['review_files']['raw']}) · [审计元数据]({adoption['review_files']['metadata']}) · [helper源码]({adoption['review_files']['helper']}) · [完整恢复树／210题失败边界／原始后审授权]({adoption['public_tree']}) · [GEN两次选择登记](evidence/chexbench/failure_events/adoption_1_registration.json)。各组`failure_events/analysis_unique_adoption_amendment.json`保留实际激活点、原提示／说明及保护事件清单。只有最后adoption_1的controller与三组原runner实际原始退出0，全部历史1保持；没有技术空答或effective-exit。

实际用户指定DeepSeek原生Claude Code结果审阅已完成：[原文]({review['paths']['raw']}) · [元数据]({review['paths']['metadata']}) · [结果到声明]({review['paths']['claims']})。CLI返回别名{', '.join(m['modelUsage'])}，{m['duration_ms']}ms／{m['turns']}轮，CLI估算USD={m.get('CLI_estimated_USD')}，不是发票，也不与CNY代理相加。服务别名不构成权重身份鉴证；保留任何负向或部分支持结论，执行ready分数不代替结果审阅。不存在独立完整性／临床审计通过声明。
```

```python
def require_base(expected):
    local, remote = head(), remote_main()
    if local != expected or remote != expected:
        raise ValueError(f'base_changed: expected={expected} local={local} remote_main={remote}; inspect before proceeding')

def adoption_recovery():
    """Gate final publication on two actual choices and the deferred actual full audit."""
    recovery = REPORT/'evidence/recovery/adoption_1'
    if not recovery.exists():
        return False
    # Check the latest attempt before opening any result or attribution proof.
    for name in ('controller', *DATASETS):
        if (recovery/(name+'.exitcode')).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').strip() != '0':
            raise ValueError('actual_final_adoption_exit_zero_required:'+name)
    for ds in DATASETS:
        evidence = REPORT/'evidence'/ds
        for path in (evidence/'generation.exitcode', evidence/'job_records/controller.exitcode', evidence/'job_records'/(ds+'.exitcode')):
            if path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').strip() != '0':
                raise ValueError('actual_final_adoption_dataset_exit_zero_required:'+ds)
        queue = read(evidence/'job_records/queue_status.json')

                or list(queue.get('datasets', [])) != list(DATASETS)):

def ready():

# Actual source line 860
def outgoing_report_paths():
    """New/modified report files only; committed evidence is already published."""
    modified = names(command(['git', 'diff', '--name-only', '-z', 'HEAD', '--', REPORT_REL]))
    untracked = names(command(['git', 'ls-files', '--others', '-z', '--', REPORT_REL]))
    return [ROOT/relative for relative in sorted(modified|untracked)
            if (ROOT/relative).is_file()]

# Actual source line 889
def prepare(args):
    if MANIFEST.exists() or (RECEIPTS/'allowlist.json').exists():
        raise ValueError('publication_already_prepared')
    completed = ready()  # No manifest/receipt is written until actual docs exist.
    require_index_scope()
    if staged_paths():
        raise ValueError('preexisting_staged_work')
    require_base(args.expected_base)
    old = tracked_paths(); needed = referenced_originals()-old
    if needed and not args.include_untracked_originals:
        raise ValueError('referenced_original_images_not_tracked; use prepare --include-untracked-originals for the explicit referenced files')
    originals = needed if args.include_untracked_originals else set()
    required = SCRIPTS+INDEXES+REFINE_LOGS
    for relative in required:
        if not validate_relative(relative).is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(relative)
    values = set(required) | originals
    values.update(path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix() for path in REPORT.rglob('*') if path.is_file())
    values.add(MANIFEST.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()); values = sorted(values)
    for relative in values:
        validate_relative(relative, originals)
    write(MANIFEST, {'scope': 'Completed, separately reported CheXbench/SLAKE/VQA-RAD online feedback and Skill experiments',
                     'status': 'allowlist_prepared', 'files': values, 'allowlist_count': len(values),
                     'datasets': completed, 'new_inference_scoring_or_export': False,
                     'cross_dataset_feedback_shared': False, 'independent_848_case_pool_claim': False})
    write(RECEIPTS/'allowlist.json', values)
    write(RECEIPTS/'prepared.json', {'expected_base': args.expected_base, 'allowlist_count': len(values),
                                   'new_original_images': sorted(originals),
                                   'prepared_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()})
    print(json.dumps({'status': 'prepared', 'allowlist_count': len(values), 'new_original_images': len(originals)}))

# Actual source line 921
def check(args):
    completed = ready(); values = paths(); allowed = set(values); old = tracked_paths()
    published_paths = old | allowed
    modified = names(command(['git', 'diff', '--name-only', '-z', 'HEAD']))
    check_values = [relative for relative in values if relative not in old or relative in modified]
    originals = set(read(RECEIPTS/'prepared.json')['new_original_images'])
    if originals != referenced_originals()-old:
        raise ValueError('referenced_untracked_original_allowlist_changed')
    assets = {}
    for relative in old:
        if relative.startswith('reports/') and '/assets/' in relative:
            assets.setdefault('assets/'+relative.rsplit('/assets/', 1)[1], []).append(relative)
    counts = {'json_files': 0, 'jsonl_rows': 0, 'markdown_links': 0,
              'image_references': 0, 'actual_request_images': 0, 'historical_image_references': 0}

    def asset(value, document, actual=False):
        if not isinstance(value, str) or value.startswith(('http:', 'https:', 'data:')):
            if actual:
                raise ValueError('unexported_actual_request_image:'+str(document))
            return
        if len(value) > 4096 or '\n' in value or Path(value).suffix.lower() not in IMAGE_SUFFIXES|{'.bin'}:
            if actual:
                raise ValueError('actual_request_image_path_not_recognized:'+str(value))
            return
        if not actual and not value.startswith(('../', './', 'assets/', 'invalid_request_image_bytes/')):
            return
        target = (document.parent/value).resolve()
        if not target.is_relative_to(ROOT.resolve()):
            raise ValueError('asset_link_outside_repository:'+value)
        relative = target.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        if target.is_file() and relative in published_paths:
            counts['image_references'] += 1
            return
        if actual:
            raise ValueError('actual_request_image_link_missing:'+value)
        # Inherited historical metadata retains its earlier document base.
        candidates = []
        match = re.fullmatch(r'(?:\.\./)+(cxr_[^/]+/.+)', value)
        if match:
            candidates.append('reports/'+match.group(1))
        if value.startswith('assets/'):
            candidates.extend(assets.get(value, []))
        if not any(candidate in old and (ROOT/candidate).is_file() for candidate in candidates):
            raise ValueError('historical_image_reference_missing:'+document.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()+':'+value)
        counts['historical_image_references'] += 1

    def walk(value, document):
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            for key, item in value.items():
                if key == 'image_url':
                    url = item.get('url') if isinstance(item, dict) else item
                    if document.name == 'request.json' and document.is_relative_to(REPORT/'evidence'):
                        asset(url, document, actual=True); counts['actual_request_images'] += 1
                walk(item, document)
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            for item in value:
                walk(item, document)
        elif isinstance(value, str):
            asset(value, document)

    for relative in check_values:
        path = validate_relative(relative, originals)
        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size >= 100_000_000:
            raise ValueError('missing_or_above_hosting_file_limit:'+relative)
        if path.suffix.lower() not in TEXT_SUFFIXES:
            continue
        text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
        if SECRET.search(text):
            raise ValueError('credential_pattern:'+relative)
        for match in re.finditer(r'"(?:api_key|authorization|password|client_secret|access_token)"\s*:\s*"([^"\n]+)"', text, re.I):
            if match.group(1) not in ('[REDACTED]', 'REDACTED', '***'):
                raise ValueError('credential_value:'+relative)
        if path.suffix.lower() == '.json':
            value = json.loads(text); counts['json_files'] += 1
            if path.is_relative_to(REPORT/'evidence'):
                walk(value, path)
        elif path.suffix.lower() == '.jsonl':
            for line in text.splitlines():
                if line.strip():
                    value = json.loads(line); counts['jsonl_rows'] += 1
                    if path.is_relative_to(REPORT/'evidence'):
                        walk(value, path)
        elif path.suffix.lower() == '.md' and (path.is_relative_to(REPORT) or relative in INDEXES+REFINE_LOGS):
            for value in re.findall(r'\[[^\]]*\]\(([^)]+)\)', text):
                value = value.strip('<>')
                if urlsplit(value).scheme or value.startswith('#'):
                    continue
                target = (path.parent/unquote(value.split('#', 1)[0])).resolve()
                if not target.is_relative_to(ROOT.resolve()) or not target.exists():
                    raise ValueError('markdown_link_missing:'+relative+':'+value)
                if target.is_file() and target.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix() not in published_paths:
                    raise ValueError('markdown_link_unpublished:'+relative+':'+value)
                counts['markdown_links'] += 1
    result = {'status': 'outgoing_content_checks_passed', 'allowlist_count': len(values), **counts,
              'checked_new_or_modified_files': len(check_values),
              'unchanged_committed_files_reused_without_rescan': len(values)-len(check_values),
              'datasets': completed, 'new_original_images': len(originals),
              'credentials_excluded': True, 'unrelated_user_edits_excluded': True,
              'new_inference_scoring_or_export': False}
    write(RECEIPTS/'content_check.json', result)
    print(json.dumps(result))

# Actual source line 1024
def code_whitespace_check():
    # Archived native sources, raw logs and reviewer replies retain their
    # original blank lines. Formatting checks apply to active code and indexes.
    command(['git', '-c', 'core.whitespace=-blank-at-eol,-blank-at-eof',
             'diff', '--cached', '--check', '--',
             *SCRIPTS, *INDEXES, *REFINE_LOGS, REPORT_REL+'/README.md'])

# Actual source line 1032
def stage(args):
    ready(); require_index_scope(); values = paths(); allowed = set(values)
    if not (RECEIPTS/'content_check.json').is_file():
        raise ValueError('run_content_check_first')
    if not staged_paths() <= allowed:
        raise ValueError('unrelated_preexisting_staged_work')
    pathspec = RECEIPTS/'git_pathspec.nul'
    pathspec.write_bytes(b'\0'.join(value.encode('utf-8') for value in values)+b'\0')
    command(['git', '--literal-pathspecs', 'add', '-f',
             '--pathspec-from-file='+str(pathspec), '--pathspec-file-nul'])
    staged = staged_paths()
    if not staged <= allowed or not set(SCRIPTS) <= staged|tracked_paths():
        raise ValueError('staged_paths_not_expected_allowlist')
    manifest_relative = MANIFEST.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(); outgoing = sorted(staged|{manifest_relative})
    write(MANIFEST, {'scope': 'Completed R49 per-dataset online same-image truth feedback and window Skill learning',
                     'status': 'explicit_git_index_allowlist', 'git_index_paths': outgoing,
                     'git_index_path_count': len(outgoing), 'allowlist_count': len(values),
                     'file_limit_bytes': 100_000_000, 'new_paid_work_from_publication': 0,
                     'user_unrelated_edits_excluded': True, 'online_ground_truth_feedback_advantage': True,
                     'standard_closed_test_SOTA_claim': False, 'clinical_causality_claim': False,
                     'cross_dataset_feedback_shared': False, 'independent_848_case_pool_claim': False})
    command(['git', 'add', '--', manifest_relative])
    if staged_paths() != set(outgoing):
        raise ValueError('final_index_paths_differ_from_manifest')
    # Preserve deliberate two-space hard breaks in raw DeepSeek review Markdown.
    code_whitespace_check()
    write(RECEIPTS/'stage.json', {'git_index_paths': outgoing, 'git_index_path_count': len(outgoing),
                                 'unrelated_user_edits_staged': False})
    print(json.dumps({'status': 'staged_explicit_allowlist', 'git_index_path_count': len(outgoing)}))

# Actual source line 1063
def commit_push(args):
    prepared = read(RECEIPTS/'prepared.json'); staged = read(RECEIPTS/'stage.json')
    base = prepared['expected_base']; receipt = RECEIPTS/'commit.json'
    if receipt.exists():
        commit = read(receipt)['commit']
        if head() != commit or remote_main() not in (base, commit):
            raise ValueError('resume_commit_or_remote_changed')
    else:
        ready(); require_base(base)
        if staged_paths() != set(staged['git_index_paths']):
            raise ValueError('index_changed_after_explicit_stage')
        code_whitespace_check()
        command(['git', 'commit', '-m', args.message]); commit = head()
        if command(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD^']).decode().strip() != base:
            raise ValueError('unexpected_commit_parent')
        committed = names(command(['git', 'diff-tree', '--no-commit-id', '--name-only', '-r', '-z', commit]))
        if committed != set(staged['git_index_paths']):
            raise ValueError('commit_path_membership_differs_from_explicit_stage')
        write(receipt, {'commit': commit, 'base': base, 'git_index_path_count': staged['git_index_path_count']})
    if remote_main() != commit:
        command(['git', 'push', 'origin', 'HEAD:refs/heads/main'])
    print(json.dumps({'status': 'commit_pushed_verify_next', 'commit': commit}))
```
